## 2026.10.5


### v2026.10.5

For formatted release notes, [read this release on the docs site](https://docs.openclaw.ai/releases/2026.10.5).

OpenClaw 2026.10.5 refreshes the sidebar, adds saved versions and Undo to Skill Workshop, and lets people read published conversations at their normal chat links.

This release covers **2,250 pull requests, 411 direct commits, and 637 contributors.**

#### Sidebar

The sidebar brings larger agent lists into readable rows, with search and pinning to keep the agents you use within reach. Subagents and Processes panels sit beside the parent conversation, so you can check delegated work, inspect recent command output, or stop a task without losing your place.



##### Sidebar navigation, organization, and work panels


- Choose any named agent from either sidebar view while keeping main-chat status readable. #### Skill Workshop 2.0

Skill Workshop lets you review how a learned skill has changed and use Undo to restore an earlier version. Its library brings editing, version comparisons, and archive/restore into the same place, so you can inspect and revise the procedures your assistant learns.

Background learning sends conversation and tool content to your configured model provider and can add usage charges. Choose **Off** to stop background learning and unused-skill cleanup; you can still request learning with **`/learn`**.



##### Workshop editing, learning controls, and saved versions


Existing Auto and unset automatic-learning settings are preserved. Legacy proposal modes move to Off, and a setting already changed to Off stays off until you change it. The library keeps up to ten saved versions; new sessions pick up skill edits. Supported unused-skill cleanup archives skills after 30 days and is disabled for Codex. Restoring pre-upgrade proposal history requires its matching backup; pending proposals are exported inactive.

- Skill Workshop saves learned procedures directly, with change history, saved versions and Undo. #### Public conversation links

A published conversation now opens at its normal chat address. Anyone with the link can read the transcript without signing in; signed-in readers use the same link with their existing permissions.

Review the conversation before publishing. **The link exposes existing and future user messages and final replies to anyone who has it.** Redaction is best effort, and turning sharing off cannot recall saved copies.



##### Conversation links, access, and public sharing


Deployments behind a login proxy need the [public-reader route setup](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/urls#login-proxy-deployment), with private app and API routes kept protected.

- Long shared-conversation titles and messages avoid broken characters where shortening crosses an emoji boundary. [#141240](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141240) Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee).
- Opening session sharing no longer waits for unrelated session display refreshes after a model catalog change. [#165344](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165344) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/diagnostics) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Published conversations use their normal chat address for anonymous read-only sharing, with optional publication of eligible X work sessions. [#166049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166049) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/urls#public-session-transcripts) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/urls#login-proxy-deployment) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/multi-user#world-readable-session-links) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Signed-in token/password users can reload, bookmark and open chat links in new tabs without an extra login click, preserving drafts and presentation parameters. [#166783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166783) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/urls) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/security-model) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Sharing requests recheck current permissions and conversation identity before returning a public link. [#167504](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167504) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

#### Conversations and workspaces

Recovery fixes help interrupted chats resume after restart and return delegated results to the parent conversation. Stop also reaches a request's running commands and queued follow-ups, while long chats need less repeated preparation before replies and searches.

Save work before lowering managed-worktree limits. Cleanup can remove local changes, including in nested checkouts, and recovery snapshots are best effort. Manual **`--max-lines`** trimming permanently deletes removed transcript text without a backup archive.



##### Conversation recovery, follow-ups, history, and workspaces


- Streamed text avoids a tiny trailing message at an exact size boundary. [0427411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/04274119f651594b74d581ae452dc8ab36d30889).
- Supporting history integration keeps saved corrections from overwriting a newer edit or another message while preserving later additions. [1cd6492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1cd64928a36671d63aea47d70faf441deef2a2a1)
- Conversation history avoids a read failure when replies start concurrently. [20ef748](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/20ef7487dd5e1267101689eeca8ab04f0944fe9b)
- Transcript search can recover from failed cleanup without trusting checks from a database that has been replaced. [2839466](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2839466d883685ce924a2b3b14c0780c2c3fc360)
- Newly created databases remain ready through worker handoffs, and replacement checks retire obsolete copies reached through alternate paths. [400bab4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/400bab4a7937e6e12a742ef4bb7121d7f9b852eb).
- Long-running OpenClaw processes release memory held by completed database-opening requests, even when reusing database workers. [4db0224](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4db022470af693f9f78dafd2a39338d5cd7dadda).
- A closed database deleted from disk can be recreated through a directory alias. [58e1c3f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/58e1c3f789ad14c62dd09603dd1d3d8bd3841fd1).
- Avoid duplicate remote-worker answers and accept follow-ups after cancellation [5c231631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5c231631d59eade7b489a902f5b49e79b55d9158)
- Rejected worker transcript saves stop with a visible error instead of repeatedly reconnecting without progress. [5dcf668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5dcf668765dea9dfc8101e163804c77ca52bdb53) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/troubleshooting)
- Supporting conversation integration uses current saved history for replay and delivery and keeps meeting exports consistent during simultaneous edits. [66471ca](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/66471cad80ee9fdef9dacff66319c008543026db)
- Finding saved conversations avoids an extra database hold that could interrupt recovery reads. [7294c69](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7294c691f2ce7a0a26191faff4a44cf32a3212bb)
- Keep transcript and trace storage in the intended Gateway location [737560a5](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/737560a532d5eea1b4450c802696a6f716cfdd44)

  Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).

- Supporting worker recovery lets eligible interrupted agent work resume when its worker disappears. [740564a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/740564a40f86deaa42de279465216c9eb587adbf)
- Messages queued during worker setup can proceed when setup finishes or the preceding turn updates. [7ecc80a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7ecc80a457366091dd61448bcc319cf1a82a5da9) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access)

- Record reply cancellation before session deletion retires the reply, giving connected clients deletion as its stop reason. [9564061](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9564061ab79c433d2d0c9f3fc902e3ccd579a8cb).
- Supporting workspace integration keeps inspection and cleanup with the recorded owner and lets temporary transcript-save refusals be retried. - Replies continue after automatic dashboard session resets without title generation blocking them. [#162689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162689) Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [@dimonnld](https://github.com/dimonnld), [@OmniScaleMatt](https://github.com/OmniScaleMatt), [@gloria0021](https://github.com/gloria0021), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- An agent turn can continue without a resend when its database worker exits before dispatch. [#163258](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163258) Thanks [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hawkit](https://github.com/hawkit).
- Malformed file-edit requests receive a clear JSON correction before edits run. [#163577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163577) Thanks [@GoldArowana](https://github.com/GoldArowana), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
- Multi-step agent work gets a bounded chance to continue after a later tool call is rejected as malformed. [#164274](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164274) Thanks [@fransqaas](https://github.com/fransqaas), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Parent agents can finish work triggered by a subagent result without the delivery deadline cancelling work already started. [#164282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164282) Thanks [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Session operations avoid repeating already validated database schema checks. [#164490](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164490) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Quoted replies load their previews with chat history, and links to older originals keep navigating when rendering is delayed. [#164605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164605) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Covered failed worktree setup can clean its empty branch so the same session can retry, and delayed heartbeat wakeups avoid false failures. [#164615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164615) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A queued message can continue after the covered interrupted Control UI run, while unsafe ownership conflicts show understandable retry guidance. [#164656](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164656) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@LivenseAI](https://github.com/LivenseAI), [@lucascmg-vx](https://github.com/lucascmg-vx).
- Timed-out or killed child tasks keep their recorded status and return results from the right owner, even when agents reuse a session name. [#164674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164674) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Agents can send Ctrl+Space and modified F1–F12 correctly to interactive terminal programs. [#164688](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164688) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Agent patches match en quad and em quad spaces without changing preserved file text. [#164742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164742) Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Durable conversation rewind and branch switching perform their main history mutation work in the database worker. [#164753](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164753) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed worktree cleanup runs as a background job, with a configurable global checkout cap and eviction policy. [#164758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164758) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees#capacity-and-eviction) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-runtime#worktreemaxcount) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- View-only subagent chats hide saved-draft recovery controls while parent drafts remain available. [#164766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164766) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Commands containing NUL are rejected before helpers start or an existing scoped command is cancelled. [#164770](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164770) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd), [@IWhatsskill](https://github.com/IWhatsskill), [@chabal2020](https://github.com/chabal2020).
- Preparing and finishing conversations moves more saved-data reads into background workers. [#164779](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164779) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- At shutdown, OpenClaw waits for managed-worktree cleanup it has already accepted to finish before closing background workers. Saved cleanup data stays available for recovery if a write's outcome is uncertain. [#164824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164824) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reopening or reloading an active Claude CLI session preserves the original elapsed Working time, including preparation before handoff. [#164873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164873) Thanks [@rogeriochaves](https://github.com/rogeriochaves), [@llmsommelier](https://github.com/llmsommelier), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Session setup, deletion, and history changes move selected database work into the background. [#164922](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164922) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed-worktree sessions archive promptly while checkout cleanup continues in the background; unarchiving waits for any in-flight snapshot and restores saved work. [#164938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164938) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Selected conversation-history checks run through the existing background database reader. [#164989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164989) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/compatibility-policy#reply-run-start-transcript-facts) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- First-use session preparation leaves the Gateway available while session data is read. [#165027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165027) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session checks do less blocking Gateway work while replies use current session and transcript state. [#165028](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165028) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Artifact downloads in long conversations can start sooner without checking unrelated messages first. [#165030](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165030) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/media-playback) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Progress-card updates avoid repeated database setup work. [#165081](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165081) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preparing model context avoids repeatedly decompressing the same history messages. [#165086](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165086) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recovering older results and checking duplicate messages avoids unrelated compressed history reads. [#165089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165089) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Less database overhead when registering or settling groups of subagents. [#165098](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165098) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restoring a saved chat position avoids unnecessary pane redraws while messages finish laying out. [#165101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165101) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Yuyorman](https://github.com/Yuyorman).
- Completed resumed chats clear stuck working timers and Stop buttons automatically while keeping unsent drafts. [#165108](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165108) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- A message arriving as the prior turn finishes avoids a spurious placement error and resend request. [#165166](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165166) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Canceling one queued WebChat message no longer makes unrelated queued messages fail and retry. [#165168](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165168) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Missing-summary recovery can return a useful task summary when automatic compaction previously caused it to reject its own result. [#165179](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165179) Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@johan-eilertsen](https://github.com/johan-eilertsen), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Reply finalization and routine session checks avoid loading large saved prompts and diff data they do not need. [#165181](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165181) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Routine session-start and completion saves run off the Gateway main thread while accepted writes settle during shutdown. [#165193](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165193) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Queued messages from multiple clients route run timing, model information and live tool activity to the client that sent each message. [#165194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165194) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat turns no longer fail merely because unrelated sessions start or finish while maintenance runs. [#165196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165196) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed worktree cleanup uses incremental Git maintenance and suspends failing repositories so cleanup and other repositories can continue. [#165204](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165204) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Status, Side chat, and progress reads run in the background, discard superseded results, and finish accepted summary writes during shutdown. [#165205](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165205) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/how-to-migrate) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Publish PR cards clear stale worktree-owner errors after cleanup and show compact status labels with expandable recovery details. [#165219](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165219) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large-history attachment cleanup and context reports use smaller reads, reducing per-request memory spikes and time holding the history worker. [#165221](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165221) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Progress images and attachments from session-bound recovered or internally dispatched agent runs survive scratch-file cleanup and chat reload. [#165275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165275) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Workboard opens its Board agent in a dedicated dock conversation without duplicating the ordinary session list or inflating people counts. [#165319](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165319) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/workboard) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/feature-plugins) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/rpc-session-control) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible Control UI turns interrupted by a Gateway restart resume after startup, with repair for sessions left stuck running by this specific cleanup race. [#165333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165333) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Slack and Discord sessions no longer show header buttons that open their source conversation; sessions and transcripts remain readable. [#165334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165334) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/feature-plugins) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-channel-plugins) · Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Outdated subagent records no longer block current siblings and nested children from settling after a restart so their parent can continue. [#165335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165335) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed chat steps stay counted when their only nested work is a hidden routine progress update. [#165358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165358) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Starting ordinary chats moves selected-session metadata rereads off the Gateway main thread while retaining current permissions and stale-read checks. [#165360](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165360) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Permission and skill checks run in the background while messages sent during active work keep their order and avoid duplicate submission after uncertain acceptance. [#165370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165370) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/how-to-migrate#await-reply-tool-authority) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/compatibility-policy) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat keeps delegated-work waiting status beneath the reply, shows remaining subagents and preserves opened tool details through handoff refreshes. [#165378](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165378) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Active tools retain live progress after chat history refresh instead of being replaced by a misleading unknown outcome. [#165392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165392) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session setup and status recover missing start or completion times from saved history in the background without changing reset decisions. [#165401](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165401) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration#session-reset-freshness) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Named Windows-1252 text uploads preserve accented words in model context when the sender omits a specific content type. [#165405](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165405) Thanks [@Chessing234](https://github.com/Chessing234), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Opening a session from an excluded agent store promptly reports not found instead of repeatedly asking clients to reload. [#165408](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165408) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
- Chat activity outcome badges stay on one line while long command headlines truncate cleanly. [#165415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165415) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed chat steps keep their titles and open their details in one click. [#165426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165426) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- A steer sent before a reply target exists can join the first turn after workspace setup. [#165462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165462) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue-steering) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Busy session lists reuse unchanged rows during unrelated updates, reducing repeated allocation work. [#165464](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165464) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The chat working line shows how many direct sub-agents remain running while the parent works. [#165477](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165477) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Reopening branch pickers in large worktree setups avoids repeated Git discovery; empty snapshot cleanup skips an unnecessary process. [#165480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165480) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stopping a turn sends one cancelled completion without trailing lifecycle updates or duplicate finals. [#165483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165483) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/agent-loop) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chats at the latest content keep following messages from other devices while readers who scroll up keep their place. [#165484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165484) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/feature-reference) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large saved prompts cost less to validate, and eligible sub-agent starts avoid redundant parent-skill reads. [#165495](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165495) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Parent conversations recover after restart without repeating the same child-completion continuation. [#165496](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165496) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
- Pending child-agent results can recover after obsolete child records are retired on restart. [#165499](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165499) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/operations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saved unanswered instructions remain actionable when sub-agent work resumes the conversation after recovery. [#165502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165502) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repeated base-branch suggestions return with less work in large repositories when refs are unchanged. [#165513](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165513) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Steered instructions stay visible in conversation order, including saved Codex history after refresh. [#165535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165535) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Steer messages sent while a worktree or runtime is starting can guide that initial task once it is ready. [#165537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165537) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue-steering) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saved-reply and attachment corrections move database work off the Gateway request thread. [#165541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165541) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Answers resumed after subagent work stay together, with timing and known parent-run usage covering the whole request. [#165542](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165542) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Injected reports and alerts appear once as standalone notes without interrupting an ongoing reply; intentionally identical notes remain separate. [#165549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165549) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/webchat) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@legarconc](https://github.com/legarconc).
- System-agent history and resets finish saving through shutdown, and configuration notices avoid outdated audit records. [#165561](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165561) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session suggestions load with less repeated preparation, improving first and subsequent requests in a large-session benchmark. [#165570](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165570) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Admitted chat messages are acknowledged while skill preparation continues, with preparation failures visible on the run. [#165608](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165608) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/webchat) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/logging) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/prometheus) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Parent agents retry completed child work after stale-result preparation or temporary worker-capacity failures instead of losing their continuation. [#165610](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165610) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saved-chat model and account information prepares in background workers while preserving current visibility and selected-account checks. [#165614](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165614) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Long chat status and model labels avoid broken emoji at the shortening boundary. [#165619](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165619) Thanks [@nocodet888-arch](https://github.com/nocodet888-arch), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Required child-agent replies keep retrying through temporary cleanup failures within the existing delivery deadline. [#165634](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165634) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unchanged Workboard Sessions boards load again with much less repeated work. [#165698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165698) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/workboard) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat shows subagent names, current status, duration and direct session links, with separate subagent activity counts. [#165725](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165725) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Repeated branch suggestions in the managed-worktree picker can return faster for unchanged repositories. [#165750](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165750) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Downloaded attachment names preserve apostrophes at the start or end. [#165754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165754) Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Repeated file listings from an unchanged conversation reuse their summaries instead of decoding the transcript again. [#165759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165759) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Queued follow-up messages wait for the original reply to finish when only its source operation owns the queue. [#165788](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165788) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Parents receive complete answers from new ACP and CLI fallback subagent runs, even after the child has moved on to another turn. [#165800](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165800) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/announce) · Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen), [@potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital), [@Leli1024](https://github.com/Leli1024), [@zyz619963502zyz](https://github.com/zyz619963502zyz).
- Repeated searches and refreshes of large conversation lists reuse more prepared information, reducing processing in the measured workloads. [#165834](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165834) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shorthand GitHub references in chat open the matching repository linked in the same paragraph, with correct previews and hovercards; conflicting references stay unlinked. [#165849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165849) Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Session diffs and pull-request statistics avoid repeated slow Git work in large managed repositories, while limiting competing content reads. [#165858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165858) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed conversation setup can complete cleanup without a conflicting database permission read, including conversations with native plugin bindings. [#165859](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165859) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Shared-conversation browsing, search and pagination use background-refreshed listings instead of waiting on slow paired nodes for each request. [#165887](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165887) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/session-share) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/session-catalogs) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Accepted conversation-history saves finish in order before the writer is released, so later writes and Gateway shutdown do not overtake them. [#165894](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165894) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sessions boards update changed conversation information without rebuilding the entire roster, keeping existing visibility and explicit stale-data warnings. [#165896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165896) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/workboard) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime/gateway-and-nodes) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat history downloads smaller ordinary pages, and repeated delivery checks avoid downloading unchanged history again while older messages remain available through pagination. [#165897](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165897) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/webchat) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/rpc-session-control) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cancelling work still waiting for conversation admission releases its locks so reset, recovery and cleanup can proceed. [#165898](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165898) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sequential conversation saves receive their own commit confirmation, preventing a later save from appearing successful because an earlier save succeeded. [#165904](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165904) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repeated branch-list refreshes reuse validated summaries and background readers instead of rescanning long conversations after every small append. [#165909](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165909) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Confirming composed text preserves the New Session draft until deliberate submission. [#165919](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165919) Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- New conversations inherit the parent agent in explicit multi-agent fleets unless a child target is selected. [#165929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165929) Thanks [@alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@markstones78](https://github.com/markstones78), [@aldiz1711](https://github.com/aldiz1711).
- New sessions avoid spurious worker-placement errors while other local conversations are active. [#165984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165984) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed delegated results reach the parent after the bounded wait for a stalled finished descendant expires. [#165987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165987) Thanks [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@LifeViwer](https://github.com/LifeViwer), [@vinsew](https://github.com/vinsew), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), [@mgr-nick](https://github.com/mgr-nick).
- New Session returns a retry message and preserves the draft when model-catalog loading stalls. [#165989](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165989) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), [@dprev](https://github.com/dprev).
- Switching among agents avoids repeated database startup delays by keeping recently used workers warm. [#165993](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165993) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Queued messages stay pending until their replies finish, and clients track the result using the original returned ID. [#165996](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165996) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue-steering) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Simultaneous first requests for a new agent share database creation instead of failing with ownership errors. [#166003](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166003) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sending a message no longer fails solely because the previous turn or activity summary updates the same session simultaneously. [#166027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166027) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Accepted first messages survive a Gateway restart during local managed-worktree preparation instead of leaving an empty unanswered session. [#166042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166042) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Creating and updating other sessions can continue while a model or native-runtime-consent change waits for preparation. [#166059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166059) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Several isolated session worktrees can prepare concurrently, with abandoned capacity recovered after a creator crash. [#166060](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166060) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Continuing a catalog conversation fails with a retryable message when model loading stalls, rather than waiting indefinitely. [#166089](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166089) Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star).
- Background commands that redirect output to a file can continue the conversation and report the result after finishing. [#166123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166123) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/background-process) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@gkoch02](https://github.com/gkoch02).
- Late completion receipts preserve a newer child answer, intentional silence, or empty reply. [#166139](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166139) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/announce) · Thanks [@Yun-0000](https://github.com/Yun-0000), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@michaeljgood71](https://github.com/michaeljgood71).
- Follow-ups about earlier photos no longer receive missing-image instructions that undermine a previous answer. [#166165](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166165) Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@nogikeyaksh](https://github.com/nogikeyaksh).
- Cancellation confirmed during preparation consistently tells clients that provider execution never started. [#166185](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166185) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Managed-workspace checks do less database work on the Gateway; cross-process listings report cleanup candidates without removing them. [#166236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166236) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime/agent) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed subagent results can reach healthy requester chats when empty recovery metadata previously blocked admission. [#166294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166294) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Jackten](https://github.com/Jackten).
- New dashboard conversation and Telegram DM-topic names focus on the supplied first message. [#166308](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166308) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Agents stored in included roster files can be deleted while preserving the root include. [#166316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166316) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/configuration/common-tasks) · Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@jaoa12or](https://github.com/jaoa12or), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Browser chats avoid a repeated final answer after background commands resume and delegate private child work. [#166362](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166362) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Sending a message can be stored and acknowledged while unrelated history reads are busy. [#166365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166365) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unrelated session writes stop causing false worker workspace-recovery failures. [#166375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166375) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
- Session setting updates stop waiting indefinitely for a stalled model catalog and can be retried without a late change. [#166376](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166376) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/rpc-session-control) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@dprev](https://github.com/dprev).
- Restart recovery hands history and search index repair to its host owner. [#166395](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166395) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session CLI archive and delete preserve the chosen agent, and trajectory exports resolve a default or sole agent. [#166415](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166415) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/sessions) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Agent binding commands honor configured defaults and account casing, and report incomplete noninteractive creation as failure. [#166426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166426) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/agents) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Linux worker hosts can recover container sessions owned by root and return useful sanitized failure details. [#166441](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166441) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/session-lifecycle) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/troubleshooting) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stop can finish an already accepted abandoned Move, while model selection refuses a session that became locked or was replaced during preparation. [#166445](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166445) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recovered subagent failures and timeouts retain their recorded cause for the parent. [#166454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166454) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Equivalent macOS temporary paths no longer interrupt reply finalization and replay the same inbound message. [#166462](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166462) Thanks [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star).
- Subagents using plain session keys list correctly with metadata from their recorded owner. [#166550](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166550) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Control UI conversations can resume authorized automation work after a Gateway restart while rechecking current permissions. [#166553](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166553) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session) · Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
- Conversations can continue after a corrupt image instead of repeatedly failing, including after reopening. [#166564](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166564) Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mirasrael](https://github.com/mirasrael).
- Fitting emoji and combining accents stay together at hard cuts in streamed prose replies. [#166567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166567) Thanks [@rudycelekli](https://github.com/rudycelekli), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Claude CLI-bound sessions can retrieve and page around messages from before a reset again. [#166578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166578) Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Lets follow-up instructions wait for an image completion and delivery instead of failing with a session-change error. [#166597](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166597) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/image-generation) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stops superseded worker setup while retaining session resources until unfinished cleanup settles. [#166609](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166609) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Prepares session-owned worker workspaces and preserves their recorded destination during eligible recovery. [#166616](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166616) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- WebChat keeps generated attachments and their reply text in the chat that requested them when sessions are shared with external channels. [#166710](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166710) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/image-generation) · Thanks [@wsk19831112-web](https://github.com/wsk19831112-web), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ing650423](https://github.com/ing650423).
- Browser Stop and /stop cancel their request's ordinary commands even after they return a process handle, retaining output and reporting uncertain cleanup. [#166753](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166753) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/background-process) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Follow-up guidance no longer cancels running tools when its steering receipt is uncertain; input is retained without replay and uncertainty is reported. [#166754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166754) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue-steering) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Channel /stop ends the selected request's ordinary commands and waits for cleanup while preserving later work and independent background services. [#166764](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166764) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/slash-commands) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Stop retires an ordinary command's queued or running follow-up so it cannot send a pending late reply after cancellation. [#166775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166775) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/background-process) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Automatic conversation naming no longer keeps finished chats busy, and history edits wait for completed replies to finish saving. [#166784](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166784) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/compaction) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Parent Stop reaches ordinary commands retained by completed native helpers and routed continuations, reporting cleanup failures while preserving completed results. [#166832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166832) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/operations) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Agents see command execution hosts permitted for their session, reducing failures from selecting an unavailable sandbox. [#166835](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166835) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Subagent recovery and cleanup use the recorded child owner so same-named sessions belonging to another agent are preserved. [#166845](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166845) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Parents receive interrupted child-task outcomes during Gateway startup, and native-child follow-ups check uncertain effects before retrying. [#166846](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166846) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Compacting, forking or rewinding immediately after a completed reply waits for its remaining cleanup instead of requiring a retry. [#166981](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166981) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/agent-loop) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Long-running agents count completed background tools as progress even when a later model response hits its output limit, avoiding false stalled-task cancellations. [#166997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166997) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue) · Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph), [@hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Cmd/Ctrl+Enter opens the new conversation; add Shift to start it in the background. [#167019](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167019) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Simultaneous conversation-history and model-context reads can wait less on hosts with spare CPU. [#167029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167029) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large older-message pages avoid reading content that cannot fit, while pagination keeps older messages available. [#167032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167032) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reloading or reconnecting to long active chats avoids repeated commentary paragraphs and keeps new text visible. [#167054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167054) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Independent prompt preparation and reads use bounded parallel work when CPU capacity is available. [#167060](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167060) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation searches can proceed alongside other searches while keeping indexing notices accurate. [#167163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167163) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Complete conversation searches stop showing a false indexing notice in the command palette. [#167174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167174) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat turns reuse coherent conversation-history reads instead of repeatedly loading the same saved information. [#167238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167238) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Concurrent session creation in a new shared store reaches normal creation and duplicate-label checks instead of an intermittent discovery error. [#167257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167257) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat preparation repeats less database setup, while accepted Memory indexing retains its executor through provider waits and cleanup. [#167263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167263) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Saving conversations and checking session maintenance require fewer repeated database calls. [#167291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167291) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Later turns in image-heavy conversations avoid repeatedly decoding and resizing unchanged stored images. [#167355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167355) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Accepted new conversations open while local draft and saved checkout-name cleanup finishes, so the first turn is visible sooner. [#167384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167384) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation branch lists avoid reading superseded message bodies when refreshing after message bursts. [#167434](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167434) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saved conversations stay readable when transcript search is waiting on a stalled database writer. [#167446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167446) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-overview/infrastructure) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Opening chats with personal model accounts no longer waits behind unrelated database writes just to display account information. [#167447](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167447) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Long conversations can continue with recent whole turns when automatic byte-limit compaction cannot recover, while saved history stays intact. [#167505](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167505) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/compaction) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness-runtime/compaction) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jaikharbanda](https://github.com/jaikharbanda), [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
- Sent prompts and images stay cleared when a newly confirmed conversation tab is closed immediately. [#167507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167507) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Simultaneous first conversation requests avoid intermittent creation failures on fresh Gateways. [#167515](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167515) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Account labels and shared-conversation attribution require less repeated database work on the Gateway. [#167520](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167520) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/how-to-migrate) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session reset, deletion, cleanup and branch listing repeat less database work; protected-history cleanup avoids a false refresh notice. [#167549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167549) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Completed replies reach users instead of being replaced by a failure warning after a reasoning-only follow-up. [#167567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167567) Thanks [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- A saved session change stays successful when an ordinary change notification fails, and incomplete cached state is discarded. [#167570](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167570) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access#committed-facts-and-completeness) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Combined session repairs move database work into the background, keep Talk on the selected store, and retain saved-chat identity if display details cannot load. [#167578](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167578) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Automatic session titles preserve quoted phrases and apostrophes, removing unmatched quote marks after truncation. [#167584](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167584) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/sessions-and-sidebar) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway logs stop repeating warnings for old blocked context-engine turns on every later session run. [#167595](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167595) Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman), [@Clode2026](https://github.com/Clode2026), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Turns stop with recovery guidance after three consecutive identical tool errors. [#167628](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167628) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/loop-detection) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/ollama/troubleshooting) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat, history, and Board operations avoid deadlocks when database reads, writes, and usage refresh overlap. [#167662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167662) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Image bursts have more processing capacity, and dashboard asset reads avoid waiting behind unrelated compute work. [#167676](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167676) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preparing ordinary saved chat input avoids an unnecessary conversation-history read. [#167682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167682) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Finishing chat turns avoids repeated session-database preparation. [#167685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167685) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Ordinary chat preparation reuses current session facts to avoid repeated database lookups. [#167686](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167686) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saving ordinary chat turns avoids redundant database reads and nested savepoints. [#167690](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167690) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Workspace publication and move recovery repeat fewer database reads while checking current ownership. [#167721](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167721) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Diagnostic logging failures release queue slots so later turns can proceed and completed tasks retain their results. [#167733](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167733) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Timed-out history, usage, and storage requests cancel waiting host work so expired requests do not remain queued. [#167740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167740) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An interrupted task's accepted completion reply recovers before later conversation input takes over, preventing silent reply loss. [#167814](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167814) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@marknilsn](https://github.com/marknilsn).
- Typing /stop in a connected Control UI chat cancels the selected session's work and clears queued follow-ups, so they do not immediately restart it. [#167860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167860) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session details and history reflect committed changes despite delayed or lost worker replies, while newer changes remain protected. [#167875](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167875) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tool-heavy chats download shorter output previews while keeping complete captured results available on demand. [#167896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167896) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat preparation and reply inspection avoid a transcript-read deadlock that could leave them waiting indefinitely. [#167901](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167901) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Concurrent session activity avoids repeatedly copying the same configuration when preparing database access. [#167915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167915) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preparing agent instructions creates less temporary data, including after a conversation is restored. [#167922](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167922) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repository-backed chats spend less time preparing a cold Git worker before their first turn. [#167936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167936) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- One conversation's first-turn preparation no longer unnecessarily holds up unrelated conversations on the same gateway. [#167959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167959) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Required turns that exhaust retries without visible output show a saved try-again notice instead of ending silently. [#167972](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167972) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/agent-loop) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session cleanup holds the database writer for less time, reducing interference with creating and archiving conversations. [#168013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168013) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Worktree operations can recover a confirmed committed result when their worker reply is lost, avoiding a false failure. [#168025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168025) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sending a follow-up while subagents work no longer prevents their accepted results from returning to the parent conversation. [#168068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168068) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/subagent-yield-handoff) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@stackingrockss](https://github.com/stackingrockss), [@adamamzalag](https://github.com/adamamzalag), [@klabir](https://github.com/klabir), [@a1fred-ai](https://github.com/a1fred-ai), [@oldrich333](https://github.com/oldrich333).
- Messages sent during a subagent or command announcement remain queued and run automatically after its cleanup finishes. [#168077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168077) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue-steering) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chats recover from confirmed rolled-back maintenance conflicts, and model fallback keeps the original request intact. [#168078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168078) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Messages that redirect an active agent stay before the later replies they prompt, including after reconnect or reload. [#168099](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168099) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Parent chats show each unfinished subagent's name and current activity, with a direct link to its details. [#168102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168102) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevents stack-overflow errors when opening session stores for large agent fleets. [#168354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168354) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
- Keeps existing and new conversations working after restart when the state folder uses a symlink. [#168361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168361) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Resumes eligible interrupted replies once session initialization or workspace preparation finishes. [#168650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168650) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Automatically resumes eligible interrupted main chats after restart even when shutdown missed their recovery marker. [#168651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168651) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Frees node-worker capacity after a forced stop by cleaning up surviving owned child processes. [#168746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168746) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), [@linhongyu510](https://github.com/linhongyu510).
- Waits for an agent to finish loading before starting its replies while ready agents continue serving. [#168826](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168826) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xiaozishan](https://github.com/xiaozishan).
- Cross-session messages retain their sender labels; the later stable-message repair supersedes the original prompt-prefix tradeoff. [#169130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169130) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Restart-interrupted delegated work returns privately to its original parent with saved context, while cancelled work stays cancelled. [#169144](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169144) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cancelling a waiting CLI-backed turn takes effect before earlier work finishes, without running the cancelled turn. [#169303](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169303) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@changeroa](https://github.com/changeroa).
- Session catalog loading times out and recovers when Unix login-shell startup hangs. [#169507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169507) Thanks [@zhangguiping-xydt](https://github.com/zhangguiping-xydt), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Visible subagents launched during an active parent turn can reach their configured model and fallback sequence. [#169984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169984) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Independently launched agent turns stop inheriting another conversation's transcript writer and failing before a reply. [#170077](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170077) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mariorivas1](https://github.com/mariorivas1), [@ghbalf](https://github.com/ghbalf).
- Interrupted worker tasks recover before a continuation or queued follow-up starts after a Gateway restart. [#170161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170161) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/session-lifecycle) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reply text already received from an interrupted native worker can remain in conversation history with a clearer failure reason. [#170223](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170223) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Human conversations receiving another session's steering update can resume after restart when their original sender authority survives. [#170252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170252) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A follow-up message preserves an interrupted request's opportunity to resume before taking over the conversation. [#170647](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170647) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway state can stay available after sleep or backward clock changes when its original locks are verified. [#170790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170790) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [@AnvinaLI](https://github.com/AnvinaLI), [@sgh6688](https://github.com/sgh6688), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), [@andersgreg](https://github.com/andersgreg), [@shawnholt](https://github.com/shawnholt).
- Manual --max-lines trimming rebuilds retained search indexes before reporting success; removed transcript text is permanently deleted without an archive. [#171134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171134) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/sessions) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Long streamed answers that quote tool-call XML in code blocks avoid repeated processing that could delay replies and other sessions. [#171573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171573) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco).
- Manual compaction works with a small session token cap when the model has a larger native context window. [#172457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172457) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Long technical replies avoid repeated full-answer scans merely for mentioning tools or functions, reducing unnecessary streaming work. [#172631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172631) Thanks [@JasonBuildAI](https://github.com/JasonBuildAI), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Affected OpenAI live replies no longer repeat their opening because streamed snapshots share mutable text; intentional repetition is preserved. [#172653](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172653) Thanks [@Richard355168](https://github.com/Richard355168), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Gavin-ifjc](https://github.com/Gavin-ifjc), [@Lamer217](https://github.com/Lamer217).
- After a restart interrupts a quiet subagent, an affected parent can get a turn to reconcile unfinished work; this does not automatically relaunch the child. [#175278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175278) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).



#### Memory and learning

Memory search recognizes more accented and Korean spellings and keeps keyword search available through more local embedding failures. Indexing also follows provider-setting changes and plugin reloads more consistently.



##### Memory search, indexing, and embeddings


- Record memory-forget requests when a reused worker has stale database information [8f7e75a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8f7e75a6929ee7d6cf6f80de7fd13db2164061e0).
- Agents retain readable memory and profile files when workspace directory listing fails. [#107921](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/107921) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/agent-workspace) · Thanks [@tzy-17](https://github.com/tzy-17), [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
- Post-compaction context estimates include the normal prompt and tool definitions. [#141341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141341) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/compaction) · Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Skill Workshop reviews and other hidden helpers skip unnecessary failed Active Memory lookups. [#143054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/143054) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/active-memory/how-it-works) · Thanks [@RileyJJY](https://github.com/RileyJJY), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@syfvb](https://github.com/syfvb), [@diegomarino](https://github.com/diegomarino).
- Session documentation clarifies that completed manual and automatic compactions share one counter. [#149719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149719) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/session-management-compaction/schema) · Thanks [@harjothkhara](https://github.com/harjothkhara), [@ingenuity2k](https://github.com/ingenuity2k), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Memory search finds visually identical accented and Korean words across composed and decomposed spellings. [#150968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150968) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-search) · Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).
- Corrected embedding-provider settings refresh Memory Core search without restarting the Gateway or rebuilding indexes. [#151263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151263) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/configuration/hot-reload) · Thanks [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Mirksen](https://github.com/Mirksen).
- Keyword memory search remains available during local embedding failures without falsely demanding an index rebuild. [#152210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/152210) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-search) · Thanks [@synthalorian](https://github.com/synthalorian), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@clanksy-code](https://github.com/clanksy-code).
- Memory searches keep their search-time allowance while managed Ollama or LM Studio embeddings start. [#156483](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156483) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/local-model-services) · Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Memory searches through Tool Search accept their existing score and result-limit aliases. [#158632](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158632) Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng).
- Long conversations are summarized before helper-completion and approval-follow-up replies while preserving the selected model. [#163117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163117) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@AXEG0](https://github.com/AXEG0), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Long conversations can continue after an automatic summary timeout by keeping recent context and omitting older unsummarized details from active context. [#164246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164246) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/compaction#auto-compaction) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Compaction summaries can continue while producing useful output instead of timing out solely for taking longer than one window. [#164263](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164263) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-agents/heartbeat-compaction-and-streaming) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@elharonyclaw](https://github.com/elharonyclaw), [@liyangbing](https://github.com/liyangbing), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc), [@wynxo](https://github.com/wynxo).
- Active Memory status separates the session toggle, trigger-recall configuration and cross-conversation memory, and logs missing-agent configuration. [#164409](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164409) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/active-memory/troubleshooting) · Thanks [@Ayushdevo](https://github.com/Ayushdevo), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@davideditelabs](https://github.com/davideditelabs).
- Large sessions on affected rollback-journal databases can finish memory indexing instead of repeatedly failing with database-lock errors. [#164624](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164624) Thanks [@triciah73](https://github.com/triciah73), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Changing one agent memory limit preserves the other configured defaults, including the memory excerpt cap. [#164633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164633) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-agents/workspace-and-bootstrap) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Equivalent Korean and Japanese spellings no longer crowd distinct memories out of limited search results. [#164660](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164660) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-search) · Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Agents can search already-visible ACP conversation transcripts when the configured coding harness is absent from the native agent roster. [#164897](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164897) Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [@techdou](https://github.com/techdou), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Disk-budget cleanup preserves referenced conversation checkpoints whose filenames begin with two dots. [#164920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164920) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Accepted Memory indexing reuses database execution as the Gateway shuts down, preserving final writes with less repeated setup. [#164962](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164962) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Memory indexing avoids maintaining a redundant index. [#165075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165075) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes#memory-chunk-path-index-retirement) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
