- Conversation search avoids processing unrelated sessions and discarded excerpts. [#165080](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165080) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation-search readiness checks reuse current indexing status instead of repeatedly scanning already-indexed large histories. [#165377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165377) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New session writes avoid repeatedly storing the full accumulated tool-result trimming metadata, reducing future transcript growth. [#165404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165404) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session-pruning) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Manual and automatic compaction prepare session metadata and transcript statistics through background database workers. [#165406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165406) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Memory indexing uses the embedding destination instead of inheriting a recognized chat-only subscription endpoint. [#165514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165514) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/memory-config) · Thanks [@dhruv1220](https://github.com/dhruv1220), [@vit-pavlenko](https://github.com/vit-pavlenko), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Session keyword recall and memory indexing do less database work on the Gateway thread, with faster recall in a bounded benchmark. [#165568](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165568) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Memory correctly identifies cron-generated sessions even when parent links form a cycle or the cron parent was pruned. [#165601](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165601) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Memory index setup and rebuilding move schema checks off the Gateway thread; missing-index status avoids creating temporary stores. [#165611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165611) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Low-memory local-model setup now explains how to choose the existing embedding-only route for local memory search without replacing the chat model. [#165832](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165832) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/llama-cpp#set-up-only-local-embeddings) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@NovaUnboundAi](https://github.com/NovaUnboundAi).
- Conversation search no longer asks users to wait for indexing when messages are already indexed and only database cleanup invalidated the search snapshot. [#165879](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165879) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Memory architecture documentation gives the existing automatic recall threshold correctly. [#166079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166079) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-architecture) · Thanks [@n0mad-ai](https://github.com/n0mad-ai), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Tool-loop history refills leave room for system instructions and compaction, reducing overflow interruptions in long conversations. [#166120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166120) Thanks [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@igs-rogenlo](https://github.com/igs-rogenlo).
- Manual /compact lets an active answer finish for up to 60 seconds and explains when an interrupted request needs resending. [#166163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166163) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/compaction) · Thanks [@holny](https://github.com/holny), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@alexph-dev](https://github.com/alexph-dev).
- Wiki searches stop on turn cancellation or a 30-second deadline and report an explicit error. [#166329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166329) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/memory-wiki) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
- Multi-word Wiki searches avoid unrelated page reads when matching metadata supplies enough results. [#166355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166355) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
- Scheduled jobs reusing a chat stop cutting existing subagents off from memory. [#166417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166417) Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Long agent turns keep memory tools available during unrelated session activity and ordinary metadata updates. [#166419](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166419) Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Gateway warnings explain why a child lost memory after a parent reset and how to respawn it. [#166422](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166422) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/nesting) · Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Long conversations can summarize and continue through a configured healthy fallback while the primary model is cooling down. [#166459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166459) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hawkit](https://github.com/hawkit).
- Keeps post-compaction memory indexing from being canceled before it starts and reduces repeated database reads during chat recovery. [#166587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166587) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevents automatic memory-saving turns from regaining unrelated tools and changing project files. [#166650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166650) Thanks [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Memory tools can continue through routine session bookkeeping without transient access errors, while resets still revoke old access. [#166758](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166758) Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Memory preparation repeats fewer eligibility checks, and selected saved skill resources can prepare away from the Gateway thread. [#167254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167254) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- The System sessions filter now hides internal Active Memory search rows while keeping the requesting conversation visible. [#167357](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167357) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@tre1d](https://github.com/tre1d).
- Compaction references explain that a summary timeout may remove older unsummarized facts from model context. [#167640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167640) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/compaction#auto-compaction) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Background memory indexing uses the current embedding provider after plugin reloads. [#167679](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167679) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@service-cyber](https://github.com/service-cyber), [@jonathanlindsay](https://github.com/jonathanlindsay), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@aoclaw-glitch](https://github.com/aoclaw-glitch), [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen), [@alfred429](https://github.com/alfred429), [@ekinnee](https://github.com/ekinnee), [@kringsc](https://github.com/kringsc).
- Historical dreaming sessions clean up after the selected agent finishes startup preparation, avoiding misleading warnings. [#167722](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167722) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/dreaming) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Scheduled memory consolidation and accepted dream diary writes continue across plugin reloads. [#167724](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167724) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/dreaming) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New memory files remain searchable after plugin or embedding-provider reloads, and obsolete watchers stop. [#167756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167756) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-builtin) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation compaction can reconcile recent corrections and completed checks into the main handoff instead of retaining stale current decisions. [#167757](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167757) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/session-management-compaction/compaction) · Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Slow embedding requests can finish within their existing budget, while deep memory checks stop within a bounded diagnostic attempt. [#167891](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167891) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-search) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@holny](https://github.com/holny), [@fonoempresa](https://github.com/fonoempresa), [@KarbonXX](https://github.com/KarbonXX).
- Memory Wiki status reuses a valid cache compiled by another process instead of rebuilding an unchanged vault. [#167929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167929) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/memory-wiki) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm).
- Memory indexing resumes after crash or upgrade preparation without needing a search to wake it, and dreaming cleanup waits for readiness. [#167970](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167970) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/dreaming) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-builtin) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Slow models can finish Dream Diary entries using the existing agent timeout instead of stopping after one minute. [#167984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167984) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/dreaming) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@woshicby](https://github.com/woshicby), [@pfrederiksen](https://github.com/pfrederiksen), [@paterskip](https://github.com/paterskip), [@Kaspnov](https://github.com/Kaspnov), [@SWDadmin](https://github.com/SWDadmin).
- Local memory indexing uses less server memory and accepts ordinary chunks with custom and older embedding models. [#168020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168020) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/llama-cpp) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@frankhli843](https://github.com/frankhli843), [@spunzon](https://github.com/spunzon), [@Deregtx](https://github.com/Deregtx), [@jj5638521](https://github.com/jj5638521), [@Nehanth](https://github.com/Nehanth).
- Memory indexing explains invalid embedding responses and offers content-free shape diagnostics for remote services. [#168035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168035) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/memory) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jzmudzinski](https://github.com/jzmudzinski).
- EmbeddingGemma gets required query and document formatting, with a one-time rebuild of older vectors; remove any previous manual prefix workaround. [#168058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168058) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-search) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/llama-cpp) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@polley-mister](https://github.com/polley-mister), [@jared-rebel](https://github.com/jared-rebel), [@ndakota79](https://github.com/ndakota79), [@h6y3](https://github.com/h6y3).
- Daily memory-note saves reject copied blocks already on disk and can retry with only new facts. [#168123](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168123) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@bit-777](https://github.com/bit-777), [@touraround](https://github.com/touraround).
- Keeps completed exchanges in durable conversation context after a follow-up steers a running reply. [#168394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168394) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/context-engine) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@robertoronderosjr](https://github.com/robertoronderosjr).
- Retains configured workspace rules during compaction even when the Gateway starts elsewhere. [#168446](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168446) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-agents/heartbeat-compaction-and-streaming) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jakeaadams](https://github.com/jakeaadams).
- Doctor preserves original memory files when invalid UTF-8 prevents a safe merge. [#169029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169029) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/workspace-and-dreams) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666).
- Memory search and Forget workers retain less memory while preserving their existing indexing and selection behavior. [#172392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172392) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Memory indexes open when first needed instead of all at Gateway startup; populated indexes may refresh asynchronously, so the first search may precede the latest edits. [#174718](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174718) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-builtin) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).



#### Models and local runtimes

Model selection and sign-in recover through more credential and connection changes, and local runtimes get setup and tool-use fixes. Google and Vertex add Gemini 4 Argon, while Google also adds Gemini 3.8 Flash.

Rerunning Google API-key setup chooses Argon as the primary model, so check that choice if you want to keep another default.



##### Model connections, reasoning controls, and local runtimes


- Supporting worker-inference integration uses explicitly provisioned model endpoints while Gateway permission checks still govern each turn. [80d27bd](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/80d27bdf9111d1aa2d5c1334432d099a6dd4a4f7)
- Supporting sign-in integration makes account-specific models available to catalog workers already running at first login. [811d442](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/811d442afa2c1103ed10f33f3b64fb1afa7675f8) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/auth-credential-semantics)
- Overlapping model or credential changes avoid a misleading Gateway restart requirement. [ad332df](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ad332df677b4897afb22ae6af2fd68f45e35c350)
- Removing provider credentials or logging out avoids unnecessary Gateway restart recovery. [b755f03](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b755f03cd5a45596e55a4a90a54ec372a3a51074)
- Related model-discovery integration refreshes account-specific choices after first login without restarting existing discovery workers. [bbdc68b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/bbdc68b94ccf9baa2ac007964bc7db2d1541f8f3)
- OpenAI and Azure Responses turns avoid oversized-output-budget errors by using the model's declared limit. [#128282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/128282) Thanks [@parker-fawcett](https://github.com/parker-fawcett), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Together model selections using the togetherai prefix resolve through the existing provider. [#144946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144946) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/together) · Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- StepFun and Step Plan model selections accept their models.dev provider names. [#144951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144951) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/stepfun) · Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Kilo-prefixed model selections resolve through the Kilocode plugin. [#144960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/144960) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/kilocode) · Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Repeated usage reports no longer keep a stalled Chat Completions request waiting indefinitely. [#145850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145850) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/agent-loop) · Thanks [@RileyJJY](https://github.com/RileyJJY), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@elharonyclaw](https://github.com/elharonyclaw), [@anyech](https://github.com/anyech).
- Reasoning effort controls remain available after a model refresh fails when previously loaded information is still usable. [#146155](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/146155) Thanks [@ngo-a](https://github.com/ngo-a), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Provider model lists stay visible when working keys use protected secret references. [#147374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147374) Thanks [@pheidon](https://github.com/pheidon), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@kybrcore](https://github.com/kybrcore).
- Configured vLLM reasoning choices reach Qwen templates and DeepSeek V4 receives its expected template controls. [#150149](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/150149) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/vllm) · Thanks [@leapdragon](https://github.com/leapdragon), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@krell11](https://github.com/krell11).

- Silent replies and quiet heartbeats contribute token usage and estimated cost to monitoring while staying silent. [#164630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164630) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/how-to-migrate#await-personal-model-account-operations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Valid annotated OpenAI tool definitions retain strict validation, OpenRouter rejects invalid cache lifetimes, and retired trajectory sidecars no longer enter exports. [#164738](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164738) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/advanced) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openrouter) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/trajectory) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed Astra answers survive a silent async-tool follow-up and are not resent after streaming. [#164774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164774) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- TypeSafe request rejections prompt the agent to correct its question instead of reporting an unreachable provider. [#164835](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164835) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/typesafe) · Thanks [@danhayman](https://github.com/danhayman), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Kimi K3 is less likely to abandon Code Mode work because of malformed calls. [#165022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165022) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Interrupted Claude CLI replies no longer save standalone tool markup into chat history. [#165031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165031) Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ndakota79](https://github.com/ndakota79).
- Model catalog refreshes no longer hold up Gateway callbacks while waiting for database writes. [#165034](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165034) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/models) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Finished OpenAI Responses runs release conversation memory while keeping response reuse for follow-up turns. [#165142](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165142) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Longer Code Mode tasks continue when providers reuse tool-call IDs; rejected reserved node commands give clearer tool guidance. [#165210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165210) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/transcript-hygiene) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repeated provider call IDs no longer cause false duplicate conflicts in later Code Mode, computer-use and mobile UI steps. [#165212](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165212) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/code-mode/internals) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- CLI-backed runs preserve their original failure through cleanup even when the rejection value is empty, null, zero or false. [#165288](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165288) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Providers reusing tool IDs no longer cause false duplicate publications, missing tool-authored replies or overwritten client tool calls. [#165297](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165297) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- CLI-backed agents keep Gateway tools available on later turns after the first turn ends. [#165428](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165428) Thanks [@nano-richtera](https://github.com/nano-richtera), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Later CLI turns use their own permissions so session, history, and admin tools remain available after the connection's starting task ends. [#165432](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165432) Thanks [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333), [@Leit-motif](https://github.com/Leit-motif), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@XX441](https://github.com/XX441), [@aaajiao](https://github.com/aaajiao), [@alexph-dev](https://github.com/alexph-dev), [@Claynetic](https://github.com/Claynetic), [@N4DH](https://github.com/N4DH), [@Jeehut](https://github.com/Jeehut), [@S1gv4rd](https://github.com/S1gv4rd), [@arifarai](https://github.com/arifarai), [@davidevelyn](https://github.com/davidevelyn), [@marl0n](https://github.com/marl0n), [@Mydudeclaps](https://github.com/Mydudeclaps).
- A stopped Codex chat can take a follow-up without waiting for other active chats to finish. [#165487](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165487) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness/runtime-behavior) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Codex provider failures after tool work show their cause and recovery advice instead of appearing as a stopped turn. [#165488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165488) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness-runtime/replies) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Local conversation-save conflicts stop without repeating model requests or switching accounts and models. [#165490](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165490) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/model-failover) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Ordinary Codex threads are guided to call tools they already have without an extra discovery step. [#165508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165508) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness) · Thanks [@mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky).
- Claude CLI replies finish after a background agent runs Bash instead of waiting for timeout and potentially repeating work through fallback. [#165596](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165596) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cli-backends) · Thanks [@adele-with-a-b](https://github.com/adele-with-a-b), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway-relayed realtime voice can connect to a complete custom compatible endpoint while keeping API keys on the Gateway. [#165632](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165632) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/voice-and-speech) · Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson), [@starship863](https://github.com/starship863), [@wangyan411303](https://github.com/wangyan411303).
- Saving credentials causes fewer Gateway pauses on larger multiagent setups. [#165644](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165644) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/auth-credential-semantics) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Personal OAuth refresh keeps credential persistence off the Gateway thread while preserving account selection and safe shutdown. [#165658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165658) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large model lists need less repeated Gateway work when clients request them again. [#165708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165708) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Codex-backed chats exclude the bundled visualization instructions that can produce unsupported raw markers, while keeping OpenClaw widgets. [#165728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165728) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-native-plugins) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Claude CLI failures show the process exit reason and redacted diagnostics instead of a misleading broken-pipe error. [#165769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165769) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cli-backends) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gemini and Vertex AI responses that disconnect inside a streamed event can use existing automatic retry and recovery instead of ending with partial text. [#165870](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165870) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/retry) · Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
- Large Codex conversation histories take less local work to page and filter after activity, especially when matching titles are readily available. [#165911](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165911) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Worker-local inference setup errors identify what to fix and which machine owns the configuration. [#165960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165960) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/native-inference#troubleshooting) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Codex stays selectable after startup for eligible official OpenAI Responses API-key routes. [#165979](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165979) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/runtimes) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Empty model-stream keepalives no longer postpone normal timeout retry and fallback until the whole turn expires. [#165983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165983) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Irish-Joseph](https://github.com/Irish-Joseph), [@cestercian](https://github.com/cestercian), [@rmyers64](https://github.com/rmyers64), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Ultrafast remains selectable after OpenAI serves a slower tier, with temporary feedback explaining the mismatch. [#165990](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165990) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/advanced) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Status and session reports follow discovered model and runtime context limits and show unknown capacity explicitly. [#165991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165991) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/sessions) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/context) · Thanks [@snotty](https://github.com/snotty), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Codex session-catalog decoding uses less worker memory. [#166007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166007) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- CLI-backed turns can recover once when a prior reply updates saved history during preparation. [#166009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166009) Thanks [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unrelated session writes no longer abort valid Codex turns through a state-read race. [#166010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166010) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Saved-session model pickers respond faster and show recovered native runtime choices again. [#166037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166037) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/models) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Compatible Codex choices remain selectable after background agent database recovery. [#166081](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166081) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Compatible providers that omit token usage can use approximate context estimates for active sessions and compaction. [#166151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166151) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-tools/custom-providers) · Thanks [@fede-kamel](https://github.com/fede-kamel), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DigitalCharon](https://github.com/DigitalCharon).
- GPT-6 Astra receives the existing instructions for carrying out tasks and reporting results in OpenClaw-assembled prompts. [#166152](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166152) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/advanced) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Claude CLI plugin completions, including Memory Dreaming, honor the configured eligible account order. [#166173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166173) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cli-backends) · Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Fealves0](https://github.com/Fealves0).
- Affected official OpenAI API-key tool turns retain reasoning by using the existing Responses transport. [#166182](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166182) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/advanced) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@astutoai](https://github.com/astutoai).
- Claude Code login changes become available in model lists without restarting the Gateway. [#166305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166305) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Claude CLI models stop appearing twice when both entries represent the same configured route. [#166311](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166311) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Claude Code setup guidance explains primary-runtime use and canonical model configuration. - Configured models stay visible with sign-in guidance when their authentication is missing. [#166338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166338) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/models) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Standalone refreshed model lists recognize configured Claude models with a valid Claude Code login. [#166361](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166361) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Background CLI sign-in checks avoid rebuilding the prepared model catalog while preserving fresh login status and saved-profile authority. - Preserves a valid native CLI conversation after a resumed reply sends progress and is stopped or times out. [#166658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166658) Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Accepts external ACP agent model selections in config commands while retaining native fallback and inherited-default validation. [#166662](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166662) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/acp-agents/bindings) · Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@ftlc-ian](https://github.com/ftlc-ian), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Allows ACP reset-target lookup to use the default account when optional channel configuration is absent. [#166685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166685) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prevents closed Codex threads with background terminals from blocking subsequent messages, and makes stuck startup waits cancellable and bounded. [#166693](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166693) Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Restores model-catalog generation when the upstream metadata feed exceeds its previous download limit. [#166699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166699) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Honors configured OpenRouter thinking levels and model defaults on the first turn of a fresh process. [#166705](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166705) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Supporting Haiku 5.5 API and Claude CLI behavior covers thinking, conversation replay, and pricing for model access already available in 2026.10.1. [#166706](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166706) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Catalog recommendations are centrally curated; plugin manifest recommendation lists are now ignored. [#166737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166737) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/recommended-models) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Codex forks and catalog continuations preserve native conversation links, and native web search respects persistent and temporary tool permissions. [#166787](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166787) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Reopening the same model picker preserves expanded provider groups while clearing search text. [#166821](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166821) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/sessions-and-sidebar) · Thanks [@patrick-erichsen](https://github.com/patrick-erichsen).
- Long ChatGPT responses can continue beyond 16 MiB of total streaming traffic. [#166853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166853) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/advanced) · Thanks [@Jackten](https://github.com/Jackten), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Codex overload retries retain the current request and completed work instead of answering an older task. [#166868](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166868) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-agent-harness/sessions-and-results) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Baumus](https://github.com/Baumus).
- Astra can wait for parallel tool results before completing its answer, preserving the requested full response. [#166876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166876) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/models) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/tool-reference) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/tool-plugins) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Explicit configured model choices keep the requested model when an alias collides; unsupported agent-scoped model commands explain how to correct the command. [#166983](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166983) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/models) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chats keep an explicitly selected compatible provider account through temporary credential loss and restoration. [#166985](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166985) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/auth-credential-semantics) · Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- OpenAI chats can reply with affected MCP or plugin tools enabled when their input patterns use lookarounds. [#166999](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166999) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/advanced) · Thanks [@scs0209](https://github.com/scs0209), [@adamjgmiller](https://github.com/adamjgmiller), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Model pickers reuse the completed catalog without repeated discovery and share the same model inventory used for execution. [#167008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167008) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/models) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Known account model choices and speed tiers remain visible during a pending refresh; failed production refreshes can still lose those choices. [#167009](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167009) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/models) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stalled provider reconnects reach retries promptly instead of holding a turn for minutes. [#167013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167013) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sszyh2026](https://github.com/sszyh2026), [@Yigtwxx](https://github.com/Yigtwxx).
- Codex explains when failed history compaction prevented sending a message and tells users to start a new session and resend. [#167016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167016) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness-runtime/compaction) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jaikharbanda](https://github.com/jaikharbanda).
- Repeated authentication reads avoid unnecessary reader restarts when callers select the same state directory in equivalent ways. [#167024](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167024) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- ChatGPT subscription replies interrupted by connection expiry can reconnect and finish from saved conversation history. [#167063](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167063) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jackten](https://github.com/jackten).
- Refreshing model lists from several providers overlaps independent requests to shorten waiting. [#167101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167101) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-provider-plugins) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Changing one provider's configuration no longer hides unrelated automatically discovered models. [#167117](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167117) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Model listing explains rejected or unavailable provider catalogs and points to recovery steps while preserving script output. - Model choices and prices stay available while a downloaded catalog finishes provider discovery before being adopted. [#167178](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167178) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Codex shows an identical ultrafast policy warning once per stored chat instead of repeating it across turns and reconnects. [#167220](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167220) Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Model choices recover automatically after temporary provider-discovery failures, without a manual refresh. [#167230](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167230) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Claude CLI conversations keep their latest history when switching between channel messages and web chat. - Background model discovery avoids loading unnecessary native agent execution code while retaining model choices. [#167282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167282) Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
- Expected caller cancellations stop appearing as provider failure warnings while timeouts and genuine failures retain warnings. [#167303](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167303) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chats using strict model templates can continue after runtime notices without clearing their history or switching providers. [#167309](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167309) Thanks [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Opening a personal-account model list uses published choices immediately; Refresh obtains account-specific catalog details. [#167334](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167334) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preparing selected plugin model catalogs avoids loading unrelated saved catalog payloads. [#167359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167359) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Browser and terminal model pickers put current and recommended choices first, with All models and full-list search keeping other choices accessible. [#167370](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167370) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Codex connection-loss failures remain in conversation history after partial output, reloads and reconnects, with one consistent live error. - Hosted-only Ollama setup now uses the dedicated Ollama Cloud choice, with old local-provider cloud URLs rejected before reset. - Model choices appear under the right provider with clearer labels and predictable expansion. [#167438](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167438) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- xAI models appear once in the model list while existing saved selections continue to work. [#167444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167444) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Concurrent agent runs retain less duplicate model-catalog data. [#167454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167454) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway restarts restore missing model choices through background discovery without requiring a manual Refresh. [#167458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167458) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Known provider models appear immediately after sign-in and remain available while the first account listing is pending or fails. [#167460](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167460) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- OpenAI and xAI discovery can show newly listed chat models without waiting for an OpenClaw catalog update. - Newly pulled self-hosted Ollama chat models appear after Refresh while saved model settings stay intact. [#167463](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167463) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/ollama/model-discovery) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Catalog updates no longer interrupt an already admitted reply or scheduled turn before its first model request. [#167492](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167492) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/models) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- ChatGPT-backed tasks can recover unfinished responses when a connection closes normally before the answer finishes. [#167512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167512) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/model-failover) · Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx), [@aleps001](https://github.com/aleps001).
- Long image conversations preserve more reusable prompt content between image-cleanup batches. [#167613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167613) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session-pruning) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed llama.cpp setup allows healthy Metal initialization to finish instead of timing out prematurely. [#167630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167630) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/llama-cpp) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- ChatGPT discovery uses OpenClaw's bundled Codex version, reversing earlier advertisement of a newer client that could expose unusable models. [#167633](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167633) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/openai/runtimes) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Direct Anthropic discovery can admit newly listed Claude models and shape thinking requests from readable advertised capabilities. [#167658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167658) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev), [@NOVA-Openclaw](https://github.com/NOVA-Openclaw).
- Eligible provider routes can reuse the original prompt prefix when skills, memory, or session instructions refresh. [#167698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167698) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/system-prompt) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/providers/anthropic) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A corrective retry after a long model response gets its recovery window measured from the retry's own start. [#167708](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167708) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Resumed Claude CLI conversations receive updated instructions and changing plugin context while retaining their history. - Refreshed model catalogs remove retired models from pickers and recommendations while retaining exact configured identifiers. [#167855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167855) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/models) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Oversized proxy requests recover or report a context error instead of returning an automatically squeezed one-token answer. - Subagent completion keeps ordinary conversation guidance stable so unchanged prompts can reuse more cached input. - Long conversations need less summarization work before continuing because oversized histories use a bounded sample.
