#### Channels and calls

Replies, media, progress cards, and queued follow-ups reach the right Slack, Telegram, Discord, and other conversations through more reconnects and handoffs. Phone-call tasks can carry a brief, accept instructions during the call, and return an outcome report.

The first import of an old Telegram message queue skips entries older than 24 hours, including unprocessed ones. Review anything you still need before importing.



##### Channel delivery, media, follow-ups, and calls


- Tlon preserves complete link and image URLs containing balanced parentheses. [193f7e6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/193f7e62a22fe7f752741d2a8c65767ffbf6cd23).
- Incoming and replayed channel messages wait for their agent to be ready after startup instead of failing prematurely. [8e3c6be](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8e3c6beb8f658f856763e8d232e0dd82ad600a53) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery)
- Discord's editable preview includes the last character of a complete batch without an unnecessary wait. [9df0cd0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/9df0cd095766ff05864aa67aa91b627a347a4d1a).
- Feishu group subjects beginning with # keep their group metadata. [#121463](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/121463) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/feishu) · Thanks [@zw-xysk](https://github.com/zw-xysk).
- Matrix recovery rejects corrupted saved messages instead of sending altered text. [#133338](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/133338) Thanks [@RileyJJY](https://github.com/RileyJJY), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Groups receive recovery guidance when saved conversation history cannot load instead of typing followed by silence. [#135253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/135253) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/troubleshooting/agent-replies-and-control-ui) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/state-and-sessions) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/running) · Thanks [@izshaw](https://github.com/izshaw), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Outbound audit history retains completed-send records for affected constructor-prefixed destinations. [#137763](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/137763) Thanks [@teddytennant](https://github.com/teddytennant), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Overlapping Voice Call questions receive a clear busy response instead of the previous request's answer. [#145776](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145776) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/voice-call/realtime-and-streaming) · Thanks [@tianhaotian](https://github.com/tianhaotian), [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@rmyers64](https://github.com/rmyers64).
- Telegram polling starts correctly from source checkouts and captured-plugin installations. [#154286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/154286) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/dependency-resolution) · Thanks [@xydt-juyaohui](https://github.com/xydt-juyaohui), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@markfietje](https://github.com/markfietje).
- Short Slack channel names such as general resolve to their actual rooms so allowed messages can receive replies. [#155882](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155882) Thanks [@MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@jared-rebel](https://github.com/jared-rebel).
- Normal Slack logs show a sanitized reason when an unresolved mention is skipped. [#157953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157953) Thanks [@Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [@shannon0430](https://github.com/shannon0430), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Malformed LINE question-button replies no longer start unintended agent turns. [#158694](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158694) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/line) · Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Teams and Zoom meeting commands reject explicitly empty timeout values before contacting the Gateway. [#158695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158695) Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Newer Discord corrections can reach reply processing while an earlier follow-up waits. [#159265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159265) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/discord/messaging) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@aatreya](https://github.com/aatreya).
- Channel tools remain usable after the agent or operator task that started the account ends. [#159396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159396) Thanks [@ceckert](https://github.com/ceckert), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@slinkoff](https://github.com/slinkoff), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Long IRC replies with CJK text or emoji leave room for server forwarding so chunks are not lost. [#159730](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159730) Thanks [@rodricksz4h5](https://github.com/rodricksz4h5), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Telegram node-command results and error follow-ups stay in the conversation and topic that started them. [#160675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160675) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/heartbeat) · Thanks [@brokemac79](https://github.com/brokemac79).
- Agent deletion guidance explains retained bot accounts and when queued messages may reach a new owner. [#161427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161427) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/agents) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/multi-agent#routing-rules) · Thanks [@charlesBerg-ux](https://github.com/charlesBerg-ux), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Matrix warns when invite allowlist entries cannot match a room. [#161607](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161607) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/matrix) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/matrix/setup) · Thanks [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [@captain-marlow](https://github.com/captain-marlow), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- WhatsApp can deliver a later decrypted message after an initial empty encrypted placeholder instead of discarding it as a duplicate. [#162078](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162078) Thanks [@stanleys12](https://github.com/stanleys12), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@hundredhandsio](https://github.com/hundredhandsio), [@jmejia-appropia](https://github.com/jmejia-appropia), [@gabrielrojasc](https://github.com/gabrielrojasc).
- Broken bundled channel media support reports its original initialization error instead of silently losing attachment-directory declarations. [#162650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162650) Thanks [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Slack places subsequent streamed progress and answers below a follow-up human message while preserving earlier output and Stop behavior. [#163511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163511) Thanks [@Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@leovoloshin](https://github.com/leovoloshin), [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007).
- Long mostly-ASCII replies require less CPU work to split for channel delivery. [#163573](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163573) Thanks [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Slack no longer floods Gateway logs with two irrelevant ping/pong diagnostic warnings. [#164305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164305) Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@Johannes-Berggren](https://github.com/Johannes-Berggren).
- Talk agent consultations can start when finalized speech updates shared history during startup. [#164372](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164372) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/talk/realtime-sessions) · Thanks [@sunnyshu0925](https://github.com/sunnyshu0925), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@samiralibabic](https://github.com/samiralibabic).
- Incoming channel pairing checks move database work off the Gateway thread while keeping sender permissions unchanged. [#164476](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164476) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/compatibility-policy#channel-pairing-allowlists) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An outdated or closed channel setup wizard no longer starts unwanted WhatsApp QR pairing after cleanup. [#164577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164577) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram keeps a visible streamed preview until its replacement is accepted rather than letting it disappear while waiting. [#164649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164649) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/messaging) · Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@jmar5439](https://github.com/jmar5439).
- Internal relative attachment instructions stay hidden in chat replies that use carriage-return line breaks. [#164657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164657) Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Clean UTF-16 text and JSON attachments retain their declared type instead of being guessed as CSV because they contain commas. [#164687](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164687) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Public IPv6 HTTPS media references pass attachment parsing while private destinations stay blocked. [#164689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164689) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/rich-output-protocol) · Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
- Voice preference preparation uses the existing worker while preserving selected paths and fresh preference contents. [#164694](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164694) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible background-command results return to the requesting chat with its conversation history. [#164719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164719) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/background-process) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/heartbeat#delivery-behavior) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@SparcleAI](https://github.com/SparcleAI), [@arduano](https://github.com/arduano), [@igs-rogenlo](https://github.com/igs-rogenlo), [@wpu911](https://github.com/wpu911), [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Chained conversation-owned command results no longer wait for the next periodic heartbeat interval. [#164756](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164756) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/heartbeat) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@ghostllm](https://github.com/ghostllm), [@hexsprite](https://github.com/hexsprite).
- Matrix drops a pre-March raw-sync cache reader while retaining supported normalized saved state from July onward. [#164816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164816) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tlon messages turn bare URLs into links and preserve Markdown images embedded in formatted text. [#164828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164828) Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Loaded history retains useful command status and spawned-session information; channel listeners continue using their own Gateway after the starting client disconnects. [#164848](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164848) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/messages) · Thanks [@sjf-oa](https://github.com/sjf-oa), [@sjf](https://github.com/sjf).
- The agent is told when an inter-session reply was recorded only in a transcript and was not delivered to an external channel. [#164860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164860) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session-tool) · Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Jackten](https://github.com/Jackten).
- Completion checks can keep the original detailed answer when it already explains an approval request or other concrete pause. [#164896](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164896) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/progress-card) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- A completed message command keeps its result when a plugin shutdown hook times out, reporting deferred cleanup instead of an exit13 failure. [#164915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164915) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/message) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Voice Call continuation waits use elapsed time, avoiding early or overlong timeouts when the calendar clock changes. [#164951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164951) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- WhatsApp media uploads on Bun retain proxy routing and work through redirects. [#165017](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165017) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/whatsapp) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- IRC rejects oversized incoming records and spaces out repeated reconnect attempts. [#165018](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165018) Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
- Ordinary Gateway logs now explain more attachment staging failures. [#165066](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165066) Thanks [@akshatshahh](https://github.com/akshatshahh), [@someone378](https://github.com/someone378), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Telegram streaming replies keep the original message after temporary server errors instead of leaving duplicate answers. [#165072](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165072) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Meeting-library page reads leave the Gateway thread and respect access revoked while a page loads. [#165111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165111) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Discord streaming previews preserve paragraph spacing and continuous code blocks across chunks. [#165120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165120) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/streaming) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram message sends and inline buttons work after chat handoffs without an invalid inferred reply target. [#165137](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165137) Thanks [@mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky).
- Approved X accounts can mention configured bots and receive text reply chains in the public conversation. [#165138](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165138) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/reference/x) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Matrix accepts and retains valid recovery keys even when verification does not newly enable a backup. [#165236](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165236) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Removing stored X permission stops mentions and replies still being authorized from reaching dispatch or posting a public response. [#165244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165244) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Slack membership caching now uses a fixed 60-second expiry, removing the environment override previously used to disable or adjust it. [#165330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165330) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- X mention streaming uses the correct subscription token and accepts X's documented event envelope, with clearer reasons when it falls back to polling. [#165337](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165337) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- WhatsApp QR startup deadlines return their expected failure without an unhandled rejection, and failed worker reconnect preparation clears its timer. [#165340](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165340) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime/config-and-utilities) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- X accounts gain configurable API spending budgets, visible cost estimates and pauses that preserve mentions for processing after reset. [#165351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165351) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Optional X guest mode answers public repository questions in isolated sessions with restricted read-only tools and daily admission limits. [#165354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165354) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x#guest-mode) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- X streaming periodically checks for missed mentions without replying twice to posts already received, and reports repeated unreadable events. [#165376](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165376) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Matrix attachments without captions no longer pass their filenames off as typed messages. [#165419](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165419) Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- Images split across Markdown lines can arrive as attachments with captions and ordering preserved. [#165439](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165439) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/rich-output-protocol) · Thanks [@Milbaxter](https://github.com/Milbaxter).
- X guests can use hidden helpers for repository questions with the same restricted access. [#165543](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165543) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Talk finds open calls without scanning retained closed-call payloads and preserves calls resumed during stale recovery. [#165546](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165546) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delegated realtime calls can end their current call, delay outbound greetings until pickup speech, and optionally hang up after speech inactivity. [#165715](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165715) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/voice-call/realtime-and-streaming) · Thanks [@mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky).
- Give a phone call a task brief, follow and steer the conversation, and receive an outcome report; optional callbacks and Twilio voicemail handling support follow-up. [#165716](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165716) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/voice-call/realtime-and-streaming#per-call-briefs-and-errands) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/voice-call/configuration#twilio-voicemail-detection-tuning) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/voicecall) · Thanks [@mbelinky](https://github.com/mbelinky).
- IRC messages and NickServ passwords keep literal backslashes, including paths and code snippets. [#165719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165719) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/irc) · Thanks [@Sedrak-Hovhannisyan](https://github.com/Sedrak-Hovhannisyan), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- IRC login accepts server passwords containing spaces or beginning with a colon. [#165721](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165721) Thanks [@Sedrak-Hovhannisyan](https://github.com/Sedrak-Hovhannisyan), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Messages waiting on startup database checks stop before dispatch so their channel can retry, and session-sharing reads can recover once from concurrent changes. [#165738](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165738) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-channel-inbound) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Replies explaining pipe operators keep the paragraphs between them instead of silently losing text. [#165753](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165753) Thanks [@Sedrak-Hovhannisyan](https://github.com/Sedrak-Hovhannisyan), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Photos sent as WhatsApp documents reach vision-capable models after their downloaded bytes are recognized as images. [#165774](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165774) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@Takhoffman](https://github.com/Takhoffman).
- Photos sent as LINE files reach vision-capable models while actual documents remain documents. [#165778](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165778) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@Takhoffman](https://github.com/Takhoffman).
- Default logs explain why a channel message did not join the running turn and whether it entered the follow-up queue. [#165876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165876) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/logging) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@PilonQV](https://github.com/PilonQV), [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Plugin channels accept queue overrides; X guest questions must wait rather than alter an active reply. [#165965](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165965) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram progress cards stay visible while delegated work continues and the parent resumes. [#166101](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166101) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/progress-drafts) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/streaming) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/subagent-yield-handoff) · Thanks [@Kimiyu-186](https://github.com/Kimiyu-186), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Answered voice calls retain their remaining talk time after a restart; later transcript lines can resume without replaying an uncertain pre-crash batch. [#166431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166431) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Long formatted chat replies spend less time being split before delivery, including Telegram previews. [#166493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166493) Thanks [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Channel status returns recorded health promptly, while explicit live probes share a bounded deadline and preserve sibling results. [#166538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166538) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/channels) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-channel-plugins) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The mock voice-call provider preserves valid caller details so developers can simulate inbound call lifecycles. [#166552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166552) Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Skips outdated pending Feishu streaming previews so completed replies do not wait behind every intermediate update. [#166610](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166610) Thanks [@rudycelekli](https://github.com/rudycelekli), [@feie22](https://github.com/feie22), [@cas1per](https://github.com/cas1per), [@L-Trump](https://github.com/L-Trump), [@xiaobao-k8s](https://github.com/xiaobao-k8s), [@csxjihk](https://github.com/csxjihk).
- Discord account health inspection avoids loading activity and voice code that could delay Gateway connections and approvals. [#166736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166736) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Eligible long replies using native reply instructions stream faster by avoiding repeated parsing of the growing answer. [#166782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166782) Thanks [@boeingchoco](https://github.com/boeingchoco), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Telegram and Discord show useful temporary progress for ongoing and delegated work, with Discord progress enabled by default. [#166861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166861) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/progress-drafts) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/streaming) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/subagent-yield-handoff) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@samiralibabic](https://github.com/samiralibabic), [@dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization).
- Successful emoji reactions stop adding redundant work rows to Slack progress cards while failed or unresolved reactions remain visible. [#166880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166880) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@notmnp](https://github.com/notmnp).
- Long Slack and IRC replies keep emoji and accented characters together when split into messages. [#167025](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167025) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor preserves Discord owner DM targets and can restore lost heartbeat routing from matching backups; the later typed-owner fix covers `discord:user:<id>`. [#167059](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167059) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jaiden0](https://github.com/jaiden0).
- CLI sends that expire while queued explicitly report confirmed non-delivery and advise retrying with a longer timeout. [#167106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167106) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/agent) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@A1fred-AI](https://github.com/A1fred-AI).
- Messages sent into a busy session wait until its current reply and delivery finish, preserving queued order. [#167126](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167126) Thanks [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@kseczhouyb](https://github.com/kseczhouyb).
- Tlon links and images keep their full destination when an address contains balanced parentheses. [#167191](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167191) Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Pairing administration moves database writes away from message handling and correctly removes older approved-sender IDs padded with spaces. [#167239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167239) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mattermost stops retrying permanent refusals even when error details mention temporary failures. [#167244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167244) Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Feishu quotes, fetched thread context and forwarded text preserve readable mention names instead of opaque placeholders. [#167264](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167264) Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [@Raven-wy](https://github.com/Raven-wy), [@Takhoffman](https://github.com/Takhoffman).
- Channel progress from Claude and Gemini CLI runs uses familiar tool labels and preserves authored execution titles. [#167274](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167274) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/thinking) · Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Chained background-command results stay in the Control UI chat that started them instead of falling back to the heartbeat destination. [#167284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167284) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/heartbeat) · Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@oywino](https://github.com/oywino).
- Telegram and Discord progress cards remain visible during background media generation and retain a failed status if results fail to arrive. [#167362](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167362) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Telegram detailed progress rows regain recognizable tool emoji, including retained background progress cards. [#167392](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167392) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Messages and progress-card updates avoid hanging when saving a card overlaps a conversation-history search. [#167396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167396) Thanks [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Completed Talk consultations keep their completion status and duration without repeated terminal-persistence warnings. [#167455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167455) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Bartok9](https://github.com/Bartok9), [@samiralibabic](https://github.com/samiralibabic).
- Telegram replies rejected before sending receive an accurate failure notice without misleading delivery-recovery warnings. [#167470](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167470) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#channel-ownership-during-an-update) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@mgr-nick](https://github.com/mgr-nick).
- Readiness checks report terminal channel logouts and blocked accounts as unavailable so operators can recover them. [#167478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167478) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/health) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram actions honor withdrawn authorization after chat lookup, and closed transports stop queued retries. [#167635](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167635) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mattermost and Discord preserve HTTP refusals when error details cannot be read; Signal returns results without waiting for optional cleanup. [#167637](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167637) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Telegram streamed answers respect an explicit reply target even when automatic reply threading is off. [#167895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167895) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/messaging) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Queue guidance explains why collect mode keeps Discord and Telegram messages as separate follow-up turns. [#167940](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167940) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue#queue-modes) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Plain-text replies retain command placeholders and generic type examples instead of silently deleting them. [#167953](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167953) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Long formatted code replies avoid unnecessary tiny messages between larger chunks while retaining their text and spacing. [#167958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167958) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Telegram settings and skill commands wait behind a pending question instead of discarding it. [#168004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168004) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@vicary](https://github.com/vicary).
- Telegram GIF animations reach the agent as video attachments, including captionless messages, without duplicate downloads. [#168011](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168011) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@ThisisPeggy](https://github.com/ThisisPeggy), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@manus-pi](https://github.com/manus-pi).
- Telegram delivers requested tool summaries with /verbose on and completed output with /verbose full in default progress mode. [#168016](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168016) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/messaging) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Enabled Discord tool-progress logs use tool-specific icons to make activity easier to scan. [#168032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168032) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/discord/messaging) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/progress-drafts) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Persistent Thinking messages arrive before streamed answers on Telegram and Discord without repeating reasoning across answer items. [#168062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168062) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/thinking) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Opt-in X maintainer trust uses eligible GitHub collaborators' declared X accounts, with hourly revocation checks that can retain last-known trust during outages. [#168071](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168071) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x#verify-github-collaborators) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/x#enable-yourself) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Background replies stay in their requesting WebChat conversation, transcript mirrors preserve future reply destinations, and Reef accepts ordinary GitHub revision links without hash-only false alarms. [#168248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168248) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/channel-routing) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/announce) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/reef) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keeps Telegram rich-reply tables visible and correctly placed before quotes and lists. [#168383](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168383) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/rich-messages) · Thanks [@dhruv1220](https://github.com/dhruv1220), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@barry-ssb](https://github.com/barry-ssb).
- Recognizes silent reasoning placeholders even when the final silence token has punctuation. [#168571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168571) Thanks [@liwenjie200543](https://github.com/liwenjie200543), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@cipp-ashe](https://github.com/cipp-ashe), [@eonicman](https://github.com/eonicman), [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng).
- Prevents queue recovery from interrupting messages still being sent. [#169010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169010) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Telegram messages stay queued when a retry finds the original timed-out attempt still pending. [#169202](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169202) Thanks [@diregoblin](https://github.com/diregoblin), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Grynn](https://github.com/Grynn), [@travellingsoldier85](https://github.com/travellingsoldier85), [@PaGrom](https://github.com/PaGrom), [@cablackmon](https://github.com/cablackmon).
- Feishu DMs continue receiving replies after overlapping settings reload and old monitor cleanup. [#169226](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169226) Thanks [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@junniar](https://github.com/junniar), [@PratikRai0101](https://github.com/PratikRai0101).
- Scripts receive clean JSON from voicecall steer without startup chatter mixed into stdout. [#169430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169430) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Questions that pause an agent can reach the messaging conversation where the request began. [#170039](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170039) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/ask-user) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The first old Telegram spool import skips entries older than 24 hours, including unprocessed entries; review any old instructions you still need. [#170068](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170068) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/telegram/transports) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@angeliti999](https://github.com/angeliti999).
- Slow pasted Teams images get time to download and reach the agent, with a separate window for message context. [#170437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170437) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@EbulenConsult](https://github.com/EbulenConsult).
- A Buzz relay disconnect during authentication no longer shuts down the Gateway and interrupts other channels. [#170591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170591) Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@jarvis-modern-mail](https://github.com/jarvis-modern-mail).
- Stopping an ACP turn no longer cancels a separate queued follow-up's reply. [#170883](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170883) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/acp-agents/delivery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@fanmeng-felix](https://github.com/fanmeng-felix).
- Discord owners configured as `discord:user:<id>` regain voice controls, owner recognition, and inferred command-approval eligibility. [#172424](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172424) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec-approvals-advanced) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/slash-commands) · Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- IRC detects silent disconnects and uses the existing reconnect flow without a manual Gateway restart; missed messages are not restored. [#172961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172961) Thanks [@rodricksz4h5](https://github.com/rodricksz4h5), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).



#### Skills and integrations

Plugin reloads and connection fixes help tools and external coding agents keep working as settings change. Skills marked for macOS also appear in new sessions on compatible Macs.

Plugin authors need to replace the deprecated session-header link helper before **January 12, 2027**, and migrate temporary private SDK imports before **January 15, 2027**. See the [SDK migration guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/compatibility-policy).



##### Skills, plugins, tools, and external executors


- Supporting integration refreshes skill eligibility for new sessions, retains selected skill sources, recovers parent turns, and preserves supported scheduled reasoning settings. [3f9636b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3f9636b36ec742c31382e9fc4db553c21c55c5cf)
- Web-search availability checks reuse prepared authentication instead of rediscovering it. [4a63135](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4a63135c697f082b2804db044471450037681626)
- Windows plugins resolve managed and lazy imports from their captured source with correct nested alias priority. [56d714d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/56d714d0136544b5eff5aab4824fa1e35341c1f9)
- Integrations canceling transcript operations get consistent errors that retain the original cancellation details. [ea7cc10](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ea7cc1066568fbbeece9151c497ecab15923bd2a)
- Prepared cron and Doctor checks keep their selected web providers without registering the same plugins again. [f8f80ea](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f8f80eaff271049014578a54d394face26ea8d83) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/architecture)

- Concurrent GitHub previews share requests, and publication-account discovery returns a retryable error after five seconds. - Large skill-library browsing and new-session defaults avoid repeated database work and unnecessary avatar copying. [#165106](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165106) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Headless node commands report signal termination so partial output is not mistaken for completion. [#165116](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165116) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plugin authors can await complete saved-session context with source checks; the synchronous reader remains temporarily available with a deprecation warning. [#165134](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165134) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/how-to-migrate) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New MCP discovery hides disconnected server actions during recovery and restores them after reconnection while healthy servers remain available. [#165158](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165158) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/mcp) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@BorisZhangYYY](https://github.com/BorisZhangYYY).
- Windows Bun plugins load affected asynchronous imports and release retired module-cache records on reload or disable. [#165169](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165169) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/bun-compatibility) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Concurrent agents searching identical installed skill instructions can share indexes and use less memory. [#165173](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165173) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows plugins resolving SDK imports receive native Windows filenames, including require.resolve under Bun. [#165282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165282) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/bun-compatibility) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plugins containing native prebuilt binaries avoid repeated full-file scans that could cause long loading and Gateway startup delays. [#165301](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165301) Thanks [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@exGeni](https://github.com/exGeni).
- Large watched skill collections reuse unchanged directory discovery and avoid repeating escaped-path warnings during unrelated refreshes. [#165304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165304) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Team Reports leaves startup quiet and performs catch-up at the first eligible scheduled collection; manual generation remains immediately available. [#165356](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165356) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/team-reports) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Codex plugin migration honors a configured custom app-server command when installing selected plugins. [#165365](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165365) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- GitHub visitor invitations and revocation by login use configured Gateway credentials and give clearer quota and account errors. [#165368](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165368) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/reference/visitor-access) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime/gateway-and-nodes) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- GitHub publication lookup again reports an ownership error when a session's recorded live checkout belongs to another session. [#165407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165407) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Zalo and Synology attachments stay correct across runtime replacement, and Copilot cancellation can prevent a request from starting. [#165431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165431) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Incoming messages move activity-time database lookups off the main message-handling thread; plugin authors gain async preparation APIs. [#165436](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165436) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/compatibility-policy#inbound-envelope-timestamps) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repeated Tool Search and Code Mode lookups reuse prepared catalogs while checking current tool visibility. [#165491](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165491) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/tool-search) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed private portals and hostname discovery work on larger Tailscale networks, with clearer discovery-failure warnings. [#165507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165507) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/tailscale) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@redasadki](https://github.com/redasadki).
- New sessions recognize newly installed skill requirements without restarting the Gateway when the binary is already on its PATH. [#165529](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165529) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Skills retain complete descriptions and metadata when structured YAML values span several lines. [#165531](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165531) Thanks [@SashaMIT](https://github.com/SashaMIT).
- On Windows with Bun, active plugins keep using their loaded source until reloaded, including later TypeScript imports. [#165533](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165533) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/architecture) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Resuming external ACP agents avoids a scan of every retained session. [#165550](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165550) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/acp-agents/sessions) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Remote-exec worker preparation includes the runtime files required by installed Codex and other affected external plugins. [#165669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165669) Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@JakeBotFleet](https://github.com/JakeBotFleet).
- Plugin help and errors explain connection and readiness checks in more familiar language. [#165741](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165741) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Pictures returned by tools found through Tool Search reach image-capable models as images instead of encoded text. [#165762](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165762) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
- Plugin authors can await strict transcript-message preparation while eligible harness appends use existing background workers. [#165793](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165793) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/how-to-migrate#await-strict-transcript-message-preparation) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plugin authors can await upstream session-link writes before adopting, binding or cleaning up a session. [#165796](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165796) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/how-to-migrate#await-session-upstream-links) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Valid plugin skills, including Discord and WhatsApp skills, stay available while source-checkout updates retain the running tree. [#165882](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165882) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Plugin authors can prepare ACP session reads from an existing private binding; related Incognito operations prepare for a later ownership change. [#165895](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165895) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plugin snapshots use filesystem clones where supported, reducing repeated disk writes with direct evidence on macOS APFS. [#166189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166189) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/architecture) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@matthematics1137](https://github.com/matthematics1137).
- Agents using a different model can consult the built-in helper and call it again after a benign plugin reload. [#166235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166235) Thanks [@PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13), [@Colton-Harris](https://github.com/Colton-Harris), [@tyler194653](https://github.com/tyler194653), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Finished local ComfyUI workflows without media report missing output promptly instead of waiting for a misleading timeout. - Repeated native plugin SDK imports reuse validated lookup facts instead of repeating path checks. [#166278](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166278) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/architecture-internals/load-pipeline) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Background title requests avoid unrelated plugin initialization. [#166310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166310) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-agent-harness/registration) · Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@cmrstudio56](https://github.com/cmrstudio56).
- Live monitoring shows current running and queued work, while shared conversation reads wait for refreshed publication facts. - Lets forced plugin replacements activate after active work finishes when the only config notification repeats their own unchanged settings. - Restores message_sent failure callbacks after a narrow partial-delivery fallback acknowledgment. [#166659](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166659) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plugin replacement releases old diagnostic tracking and runs the retiring plugin's cleanup before registering its replacement. - Lets slow but advancing native plugin verification finish and reuses its result during model catalog refreshes. [#166687](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166687) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@martinderm](https://github.com/martinderm), [@swampgorilla](https://github.com/swampgorilla).
- Repeated plugin reloads retain less retired plugin state while active calls and streams keep their selected plugins until completion. [#166751](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166751) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Code Mode keeps core shell, process and file arguments visible in crowded tool catalogs, reducing avoidable failed calls and retries. [#166785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166785) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/code-mode) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- External ACP coding agents start under the correct OpenClaw owner, configured aliases use the correct reply account, and authorized existing conversations can resume. - Coding-agent session checks and resets avoid repeated metadata work on the Gateway main thread. [#167086](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167086) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Code Mode guidance explains how to read declaration text from the file record, helping agents avoid an initial failed discovery call. [#167286](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167286) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@rclarke0](https://github.com/rclarke0).

- Plugin builds and validation reject malformed metadata text before silently rewriting author-owned files. [#167391](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167391) Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).

- Scheduled model-catalog updates reuse unchanged external plugins, avoiding an unnecessary post-startup reload that could stall the Gateway. [#169746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169746) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/compatibility-policy#session-header-link-helper) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- MCP sign-in can finish when a provider such as Dropbox registers the same local callback without its port. [#170876](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170876) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/mcp/transports) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@9fw2pq8sgb-art](https://github.com/9fw2pq8sgb-art).
- Agents can explicitly end their active turn, reload a plugin after cleanup, and continue the same conversation with the result. [#171257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171257) Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Explicitly enabled standalone plugin files now register their Gateway hooks, including local tool-blocking policies. [#171330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171330) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/hooks/tool-policy) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-extensions) · Thanks [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- After removing a local MCP server whose executable is missing, affected conversations can continue without a Gateway restart. [#171397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171397) Thanks [@joelagnel](https://github.com/joelagnel).
- When Tailscale Serve is disabled by tailnet policy, the Gateway can keep authenticated access available on the same computer. [#171509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171509) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Eligible skills using the macos platform label appear in new agent sessions on compatible local or remote Macs. [#172214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172214) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/creating-skills) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/skills) · Thanks [@fhgffy](https://github.com/fhgffy), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jessecq1995](https://github.com/jessecq1995).
- Temporary SDK exports restore 29 affected plugin imports, including the reported Zulip approval import; migrate before January 15, 2027 and use upsertAuthProfileWithLock for the removed auth-profile helper. [#173777](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173777) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/compatibility-policy#private-runtime-facade-exports) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@FtlC-ian](https://github.com/FtlC-ian).



#### Web and native apps

The Dashboard handles reconnects and long chat histories with fewer interruptions, and terminal docks keep their tabs and output when moved. iPhone and iPad get roomier compact chat, fixes for duplicated streamed replies, and Cloudflare Access sign-in for protected Gateways. Mac fixes cover setup and reconnects.



##### Dashboard, chat, and native-app changes


- Keep replies visible and the reading position steady while live output is handed over to saved history. [1f9022d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/1f9022d2af0336ede45b249f259e820e25f0d91b).
- The app preserves its Gateway process's existing startup readiness allowance [3d181e1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3d181e1f22720fb1f994d7d0cbf9d4446277b174).
- Supporting browser integration keeps agents' tabs separate; compact iOS chat gives messages more room and preserves accessible speaker names. [3f76fe0](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/3f76fe021990df309bfcd0905030520bb30f81f4)
- Pending chats retain their normal frame and saved layout with sending disabled until the new session is confirmed. [5a9a6d3](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5a9a6d3ae51a89c5fd923c5aab83e1ba88c13a0a)
- Retired iOS Gateway connections close their private network transport. [b3a37c2](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b3a37c253b7d2e65e7926298a2b933ba8cc658fa)
- Session-list refreshes share work when startup and background refresh overlap. [d60b095](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d60b0950a2d911da471c00fdb1648f7c5d61bd09)
- The Logs page shows current logs after a Gateway restart and keeps visible rows when refresh fails. [ddd39ec](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/ddd39ecb5bc94b4c7b197c0278168c1d64d29948)
- Saved iOS gateways coordinate Cloudflare Access sign-in and cleanup across profiles. [#147238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147238) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
- Connect native iOS chat and media to gateways protected by Cloudflare Access through browser sign-in. [#147244](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/147244) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/ios#cloudflare-access-sign-in) · Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin), [@TheAngryPit](https://github.com/TheAngryPit), [@joshavant](https://github.com/joshavant).
- Saved assistant commentary appears once while a Control UI run continues, preserving surrounding output order. [#158459](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158459) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- macOS can wait up to five minutes for the same launchd Gateway to finish starting after reboot. [#158954](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/158954) Thanks [@MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0), [@cjn119-ui](https://github.com/cjn119-ui).
- Seven rare Lobsterdex visitors bring the collection to 49 characters. [#159815](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159815) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/lobster) · Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
- Removing a Settings list or map entry keeps keyboard focus in the same collection. [#160131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160131) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/settings) · Thanks [@moerai](https://github.com/moerai), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@patrick-erichsen](https://github.com/patrick-erichsen).
- Change model and reasoning preferences during a reply so waiting messages use the latest saved choices. [#160440](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160440) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/thinking) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@hxy91819](https://github.com/hxy91819), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Future timestamps keep future wording when calendar-date formatting fails. [#164269](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164269) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat file-link tooltips let users copy the full path without opening a preview, with keyboard access and copy feedback. [#164431](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164431) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@jjjhenriksen](https://github.com/jjjhenriksen), [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Windows nodes reduce reply delays from workspace controls and can run fresh commands after successful runtime refresh cleanup. [#164523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164523) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/session-lifecycle) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Review no longer attributes unchanged pre-session edits to the session in the covered Uncommitted view case. [#164671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164671) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Device-only text loads when Devices opens, reducing the initial Control UI download. [#164735](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164735) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Waiting Control UI browsers show declined or expired access requests and offer an explicit retry. [#164793](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164793) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/connect-and-pair) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/devices) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/auth) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sending a chat message keeps the model picker steady, with loading feedback reserved for the actual model catalog. [#164963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164963) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Activity recaps refresh in subscribed clients after conversation compaction. [#165024](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165024) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New session tabs no longer show Disconnected during normal initial connection. [#165051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165051) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@vyctorbrzezowski](https://github.com/vyctorbrzezowski).
- Native chat names Tool Search activity clearly and aligns tool icon meanings across desktop, mobile, and web. [#165095](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165095) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/progress-drafts) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Settings preserves composing text and lets the focused control handle Escape without accidental saves or dismissal. [#165100](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165100) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/settings) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Review uses available header space for long branch names and shows the full comparison on hover. [#165126](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165126) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Control UI metadata refreshes send less duplicate model data and avoid unnecessary projection work. [#165190](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165190) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/operator-methods) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Choose an agent avatar for the browser tab icon to distinguish agents while keeping activity and attention indicators. - Concurrent authorized connections can share successful Gateway reads, reducing repeated work and queues for status-like requests. [#165203](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165203) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/operator-methods) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-overview/infrastructure) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Talk settings text downloads when Settings loads, slightly reducing the initial Control UI download. [#165228](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165228) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- macOS session actions and sleep/wake retries stay bound to their original Gateway; logging verbosity changes apply immediately to existing loggers. - macOS respects the native Claude catalog opt-out; microphone diagnostics share local wake-word capture, yield to Talk/push-to-talk and release capture when closed. [#165318](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165318) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/voicewake) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/icon) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unusual model-provider IDs display readable labels instead of selecting inherited object properties. [#165339](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165339) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Control UI dialogs open and close without animation when the operating system requests reduced motion. [#165353](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165353) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/settings) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The macOS no-speech message gains translations in 21 non-English locales. [#165369](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165369)
- The compact System busyness widget keeps CPU and memory in the same order as the expanded view, before Gateway ping. [#165393](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165393) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/feature-reference) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Agent names and avatars remain visible while failed identity refreshes wait before retrying during Gateway restarts. [#165443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165443) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- iOS keeps spoken replies visible, preserves local-answer order, and lets large continuation notices expand on demand. [#165473](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165473) Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez), [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
- Android releases decoded attachment image memory after JPEG encoding succeeds or fails. [#165562](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165562) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The macOS CLI rejects oversized numeric ports and handles very large discovery timeouts without overflow crashes. [#165630](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165630) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Android’s accent colors stay easy to tap on narrow screens. [#165639](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165639) Thanks [@IWhatsskill](https://github.com/IWhatsskill).
- Unrecognized model, Mac, plugin and update identifiers now receive the intended labels, icons or help. [#165647](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165647) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Native iOS chat does less repeated parsing and menu work as replies stream. [#165927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165927) Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez), [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
- Native app translations use the updated degraded-check wording. [#165959](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165959)
- Expanded agent updates show one source link instead of duplicate attribution rows. [#166006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166006) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Apple patch previews and macOS failed update notifications avoid crashes caused by extreme numeric values. [#166036](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166036) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Optional lobster-pet interactions handle a browser refusal to resume sound without an unhandled rejection. [#166043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166043) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Compact iOS chat gives replies more width, preserves accessible speaker names, and adds tails to completed standalone bubbles. [#166067](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166067) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/ios) · Thanks [@Marvinthebored](https://github.com/Marvinthebored), [@Peetiegonzalez](https://github.com/Peetiegonzalez), [@Solvely-Colin](https://github.com/Solvely-Colin).
- Dictate a follow-up while the assistant responds, with normal Send feedback and no duplicated mobile transcript insertion. [#166075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166075) Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Normal shell exit closes its terminal tab and an otherwise empty bottom or right dock. [#166087](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166087) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/panels) · Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- History and session lists can load while unrelated catalog or subscription preparation waits. [#166122](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166122) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/transport) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Attention warnings clear while messages actively wait for settings or execute commands. [#166124](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166124) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Open supported running-work counts or child names to inspect delegated work beside chat; narrow panes show the work panel full width. [#166142](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166142) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Terminal prompts display bundled Nerd Font symbols, and a browser-local font setting updates open terminals without restarting shells. [#166180](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166180) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/panels#terminal-fonts) · Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Link previews consume only a bounded page head, reducing Gateway parsing work. [#166195](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166195) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/security-model) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Moving a terminal between bottom and right docks keeps its tabs, selection and output beside the selected chat without replacing the shell. [#166213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166213) Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Parent chats identify failed or blocked children, show their full diagnostic, and link directly to the affected session. - Open chats reuse unchanged command menus and model choices instead of repeatedly fetching the same metadata. [#166250](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166250) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/operator-methods) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chats following the latest message settle at the true end after late layout measurements. [#166261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166261) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).

- Native chat keeps tool titles readable and shows missing outcomes separately as No result. - Makes Linux Quick Chat respect Escape and the latest show or hide request during startup. [#166673](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166673) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).

- Gateway shutdown finishes accepted voice persistence, and post-call summaries avoid duplicate sends after marker failures and respect custom conversation stores.
