- Activity shows compact app summaries and watched conversations before expandable grouped connection diagnostics. [#166814](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166814) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/multi-user) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The mobile model selector has a slimmer highlight and more label spacing while retaining its touch target. [#166819](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166819) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Keyboard, goal and command submissions clear the submitted collaborator draft preview before sending. [#166824](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166824) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed chat images have clear previews with Copy and Download in an adjacent image actions menu. [#166829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166829) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The shared native-app runtime improves worker termination at memory limits and idle-worker cleanup. [#166838](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166838) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Holding the microphone to begin dictation then releasing it keeps recording until a separate Stop tap. [#166843](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166843) Thanks [@alfredjbclaw](https://github.com/alfredjbclaw), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Starting a chat keeps the header, submitted prompt and disabled message box visible without disrupting navigation or saved splits. [#166865](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166865) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- iOS Access settings and expired setup-code guidance gain translations in 21 non-English locales. [#166873](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166873)
- Native chat keeps later backtick code examples literal in an affected combination of inline formulas and code spans. [#166951](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166951) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- PR automation checkboxes respond immediately and remain independently usable while saves finish, with less popup movement. [#166976](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166976) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Recent projects and folders can load without waiting for session display details to refresh. [#166987](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166987) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/sessions-and-sidebar) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stopped replies keep their interruption marker in the conversation without a duplicate badge above the editor. [#166994](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166994) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Workboard downloads its full page when opened and explains how to recover if that download fails. [#167006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167006) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/feature-plugins) · Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Open chats pause automatic read requests and identity refreshes during Gateway restarts and pace temporary retries. [#167020](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167020) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Returning to a tab preserves instructions to reload, fix credentials or request pairing after a rejected connection. [#167031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167031) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Workboard's column color selector displays a readable Color label instead of a translation key. [#167082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167082) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Repeated Publish PR option requests avoid rediscovering the same managed worktree's Git target. [#167189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167189) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Opening a chat no longer briefly flashes timeout cards for expired subagent links. [#167232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167232) Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Browser tab icon settings gain translated labels across 20 Control UI languages. [#167289](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167289)
- Browser-tab icon settings and their browser-local collection explanations are translated into 20 languages. [#167307](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167307)
- Recent and discovered projects load faster when substantial session history is stored. [#167322](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167322) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- iOS readiness-check instructions and completion messages gain translations in 21 non-English locales. [#167329](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167329)
- Renaming or recoloring a Sessions board preserves columns saved elsewhere, and mistyped Workboard status filters report valid choices. [#167348](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167348) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Control UI reconnects avoid a Gateway-wide stall caused by repeatedly loading large external plugins for a cold tool list. [#167374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167374) Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Failed GitHub account discovery in chat can be retried or dismissed without leaving an intrusive publication error above the composer. [#167375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167375) Thanks [@jalehman](https://github.com/jalehman).
- Plugin catalog status dots align with the plugin icon and name, including long-name cards. [#167400](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167400) Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Control UI initial loading waits through Gateway restarts and resumes without repeatedly retrying the same unavailable connection. [#167407](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167407) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Native chat labels for background tasks and injected context gain translations in 21 non-English locales. [#167408](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167408)
- Workboard dispatch shows each failed card's reason and offers the approved admin-request route when the card and caller qualify. [#167427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167427) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/workboard) · Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [@mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- iPhone and iPad Safari show one Attach action, with camera, photos and files offered by the native picker. [#167590](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167590) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Status checks for isolated macOS installations promptly report their absent Gateway instead of waiting on an unrelated host service. [#167677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167677) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Inbox mentions stay dismissible during recap, automation-badge, and turn-status updates. [#167689](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167689) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Workspace icons load after Gateway startup, and disconnected HTTP requests stop cleanly. [#167697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167697) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/prometheus) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Talk saves ordinary call records and transcript bookkeeping in a background database worker while preserving accepted writes through cleanup. [#167720](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167720) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Firefox keeps selected agent-avatar or Lobsterdex tab artwork after appearance updates. [#167759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167759) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/settings) · Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Agent-avatar browser tab icons can use square, rounded, or circular shapes from Appearance settings. [#167809](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167809) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui) · Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Opening chat avoids fetching unopened conversations and shares overlapping app-discovery requests. [#167906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167906) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/feature-reference) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateways reuse shared update payloads when sending agent and presence events to multiple viewers. [#167961](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167961) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Control UI sends fewer idle identity requests and pauses background reads during announced gateway restarts. [#167968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167968) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repeat Control UI visits reuse unchanged uploaded avatars and retained build assets with fewer downloads. [#167993](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167993) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/offline-and-reconnect) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation navigation appears only when the loaded chat needs scrolling, keeping short chats clear. [#168010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168010) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reloading the Control UI reuses unchanged plugin styles and scripts, including after a scoped cookie renews. [#168023](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168023) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/offline-and-reconnect) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- iPhone and Mac apps recover unresponsive Gateway connections sooner after network changes and resume retries when connectivity returns. [#168024](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168024) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Themes can customize dashboard names, icons and working indicators; terminal themes use a quieter neutral presentation. - Optional question cards use less space by leading with the question and removing repeated visible instructions. [#168040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168040) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Take photo opens device capture directly in recognized Android browsers in Chat and New Session. [#168049](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168049) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/chat) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reopened dashboards show saved titles and panel positions consistently while keeping one loading placeholder until widgets are ready. [#168073](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168073) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/offline-and-reconnect) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Mac app reconnects automatically after a trusted Gateway certificate renewal, including recovery after network loss or a restart. [#168151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168151) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/remote) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@fuller-stack-dev](https://github.com/fuller-stack-dev).
- Refreshes Activity message previews when archived conversation history is restored. [#168397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168397) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Lets chats recover after a server update and reopen the same conversation link. [#168941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168941) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- File attachments finish with one usable card instead of a false error or duplicate. [#169093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169093) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Automatic recovery reloads reopen the same chat with its draft instead of a Not found page. [#169194](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169194) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/urls) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac local setup saves missing Gateway authentication and reduces runtime preparation and connection delays. [#169403](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169403) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/mac/bundled-gateway) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Control UI chats continue through unrelated pairing activity and ordinary tab reconnects while explicit revocation still stops affected work. [#169457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169457) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
- The Mac app recognizes its protected Bun Gateway command during update recovery instead of remaining on the help screen for this parser error. [#170006](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170006) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jokerman1302-eng](https://github.com/jokerman1302-eng), [@davidmackrory-byte](https://github.com/davidmackrory-byte), [@josephsilvagnoli](https://github.com/josephsilvagnoli).
- Spoken Talk requests can continue after voice setup finishes instead of cancelling immediately while still connected. [#170083](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170083) Thanks [@brokemac79](https://github.com/brokemac79).
- Queued Swarm session updates retain their publication work after task completion; the later shutdown fix waits for accepted updates before closing clients. [#170231](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170231) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Verified external macOS terminals can restart the Gateway despite an unreadable process ancestor. [#170354](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170354) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Gateway shutdown waits for accepted session updates to publish before closing connected clients. [#170555](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170555) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- macOS Gateway repair recognizes overlapping service loading after native inspection confirms the service is loaded. [#170740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170740) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Mac app recovery keeps a newer managed Gateway and can restore its missing Node service rather than attempting a downgrade. [#170766](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170766) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Open dashboard tabs stay connected when a busy Gateway delays application heartbeats, preserving pending requests. [#171253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171253) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway request failures can report an error while keeping a healthy connection open for recovery requests. [#171303](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171303) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac node commands consistently find tools in preferred folders after launching the app from Finder, Dock or Spotlight. [#171460](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171460) Thanks [@brianmcguire](https://github.com/brianmcguire), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jamiezigelbaum](https://github.com/jamiezigelbaum).
- Gemini3.8 Talk saves spoken questions alongside replies so both sides remain after Stop and reload. [#171506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171506) Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac app Retry repairs plugin links, checks the bundled Gateway and clears the incomplete-update warning after recovery succeeds; failures show the cause. [#171508](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171508) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman).
- Mac Companion setup can finish checking a saved AI sign-in after wizard replies instead of failing with expired caller authority. [#171667](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171667) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@enter501](https://github.com/enter501).
- Mac service restarts preserve multiline credentials, quotes, and whitespace; restore any already-truncated value and run openclaw gateway install --force. [#171740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171740) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
- Settings saves involving secret references can acknowledge changes already applied by the Gateway instead of timing out. [#171922](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171922) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Workspace editor saves over 2 MiB of UTF-8 text are rejected before replacing the existing document, matching the agent's read limit. [#171938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171938) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Connection timeouts show their explanation before automatic retry discards it; this improves diagnosis rather than repairing the underlying outage. [#172626](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172626) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@najef1979-code](https://github.com/najef1979-code), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A prompt sent immediately after Stop during preparation keeps its place in the queue and receives a reply after the stopped turn settles. [#172685](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172685) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Returning to a tab preserves an opening connection, and Logs resumes its saved position on the same Gateway; in-place log rewrites can still leave stale cached text. [#172864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172864) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@najef1979-code](https://github.com/najef1979-code).
- Following a large log avoids rescanning old history on each poll; this reduces Logs-page work without repairing the underlying connection outage. [#172885](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172885) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@najef1979-code](https://github.com/najef1979-code).
- Fresh Mac profiles initialize their Gateway correctly, and automatic provider setup waits through supervised startup retries. [#173246](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173246) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Downloads from the native macOS dashboard and conversation window open Save while keeping the page available, instead of replacing it with archive bytes. [#173654](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173654) Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).



#### Browser and computer use

Browser previews keep refreshing through more input activity, and ordinary tabs stay usable alongside extension pages. Cleanup leaves other agents' tabs alone, while desktop-tool discovery waits to start its helper until it is needed.



##### Browser, upload, desktop, and sandbox changes


- Chrome automation can recover on a fresh worker after a temporary harmless-cleanup storage failure without losing pairing. [#151037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151037) Thanks [@ethangui](https://github.com/ethangui).
- Finished MCP agent runs release desktop control, and rebuilding tools preserves the same run's computer session. [#151127](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/151127) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/computer-use) · Thanks [@ylcn91](https://github.com/ylcn91), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vovarbv](https://github.com/vovarbv), [@ghbalf](https://github.com/ghbalf), [@aleps001](https://github.com/aleps001).
- The browser panel keeps refreshing during sustained typing or scrolling when live streaming is unavailable. [#153580](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/153580) Thanks [@alexsimeonoww](https://github.com/alexsimeonoww), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Chrome extension uploads totaling strictly less than 47 MiB work without local-file read permission; larger uploads still need a readable path and permission. [#160013](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160013) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/browser-control) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/chrome-extension) · Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@elandesberg](https://github.com/elandesberg), [@holny](https://github.com/holny), [@Hensarj](https://github.com/Hensarj).
- Chrome extension pages and discarded tabs no longer block the listing and use of ordinary tabs. [#162110](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162110) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/chrome-extension) · Thanks [@openclaw-agent-man](https://github.com/openclaw-agent-man), [@dlmccoy](https://github.com/dlmccoy), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Browser tool guidance tells agents how to use the configured browser node rather than encouraging a host override. [#164575](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164575) Thanks [@miguelbranco80](https://github.com/miguelbranco80), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Linux browser startup finds Playwright Chromium in relative, npm-configured and package-local caches. [#164659](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164659) Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- The Chrome extension relay avoids ports already supplied in configured browser URLs. [#164678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164678) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Fetched pages retain paragraph and list boundaries in plain text. [#164692](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164692) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- DuckDuckGo searches avoid the browser-identifying header that triggered observed bot challenges. [#164726](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164726) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/duckduckgo-search) · Thanks [@takyyon](https://github.com/takyyon), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Web fetch text extraction retains literal syntax inside recognized fenced code examples while converting surrounding prose. [#164936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164936) Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Local sandbox workspace operations avoid loading large recovery records just to check existence. [#165093](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165093) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible first sandbox sessions reuse dependency preparation started after Gateway readiness, reducing setup waits. [#165125](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165125) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Quiet commands receive fewer false stall warnings, unchanged polling is detected, and stuck sandbox-control calls get bounded cancellation. [#165167](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165167) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/background-process) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New managed sandbox workspaces avoid a repeated source scan, and setup timing logs and metrics help identify slow preparation phases. [#165411](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165411) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/diagnostics) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/prometheus#worktree-preparation) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sandbox setup, synchronization and retained-workspace cleanup move database persistence off the Gateway thread. [#165534](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165534) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sandbox creation and cleanup reduce blocking Gateway database work and wait for accepted removal to finish during shutdown. [#165558](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165558) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Agents on macOS and Linux can read Windows-style UTF-16 text files that include a byte-order mark, including sandbox reads. [#165777](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165777) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Empty desktop and portal streams stop waiting sooner after their target closes, while streams with final data allow it to drain before shutdown. [#165806](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165806) Thanks [@SebTardif](https://github.com/SebTardif), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Agents stop trying managed web search before a provider is configured and can point users to setup. [#166199](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166199) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/web) · Thanks [@jacobtomlinson](https://github.com/jacobtomlinson).
- Discovers computer tools without starting an unused desktop helper; actual use still prepares the desktop. [#166602](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166602) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Enables newer Peekaboo clients to use window-specific desktop actions through the macOS app's embedded host. [#166615](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166615) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Closes untracked or older managed-browser tabs before recently used session tabs when enforcing the existing tab limit. [#166666](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166666) Thanks [@ruel225](https://github.com/ruel225), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@vokaplok](https://github.com/vokaplok).
- Browser response and navigation URL filters avoid demonstrated long stalls on troublesome wildcard patterns. [#166727](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166727) Thanks [@SebTardif](https://github.com/SebTardif), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Missing or unauthorized portal WebSocket connections return clear errors and close cleanly instead of hanging. [#167065](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167065) Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Browser management commands honor an explicitly requested timeout and reject invalid values before connecting. [#167252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167252) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/browser) · Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Browser form and batch commands reject malformed text input before changing page values instead of silently substituting characters. [#167412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167412) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/browser) · Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Cleaning up one agent's session preserves another agent's browser tabs even when both sessions share the same name. [#167484](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167484) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/browser/configuration) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed portal connections close cleanly when their worker is unavailable instead of accumulating open sockets. [#167645](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167645) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/portals) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Authorized commands sent to paired POSIX nodes work when the command ends in a newline, space, or tab. [#168120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168120) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
- Headless browser pages can dismiss invisible passkey prompts to offer another sign-in method; passkeys need a visible profile, and an already-waiting attempt needs cancellation and reload. [#170857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170857) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/browser-login#passkeys-in-headless-browsers) · Thanks [@trukhinyuri](https://github.com/trukhinyuri), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chrome relay cache settings, extra headers, and service-worker bypass survive allowed navigation without incorrectly reporting that access was revoked. [#173486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173486) Thanks [@LiuwqGit](https://github.com/LiuwqGit), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ValantisV](https://github.com/ValantisV).



#### Automations

Scheduled jobs retain saved model choices and return results to the chat that created them through more recovery and delivery cases. Webhooks can reject work when capacity is unavailable so senders can retry.



##### Scheduled jobs, exit commands, and webhooks


- Exit-triggered automations preserve escaped spaces and newer edits; reapply commands whose saved escaping was already trimmed. [#161321](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161321) Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Cron guidance explains why a trailing NO_REPLY suppresses a text announcement, including its preceding report. [#161964](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161964) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/cron#silent-token-suppression) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/troubleshooting) · Thanks [@heranliu](https://github.com/heranliu), [@jackson2w](https://github.com/jackson2w), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Viewing a scheduled run transcript transfers less unnecessary history data internally. [#163885](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163885) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Automations model suggestions follow a changed selected agent without losing the draft or All agents scope. [#164357](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164357) Thanks [@TUARAN](https://github.com/TUARAN), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Automations keeps same-named models from different providers distinct and saves the chosen provider. [#164574](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164574) Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Cron permission updates publish in order, expiring standing grants are rechecked, and accepted persistence settles during shutdown. [#164805](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164805) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Workboard refuses to redispatch a retained checkout with a different explicit base ref, preserving work and explaining which refs differ. [#164829](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164829) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@ArcueidMP](https://github.com/ArcueidMP), [@mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Workboard avoids repeatedly downloading unchanged cards while keeping live edits current. [#165058](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165058) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor-migrated heartbeat tasks for secondary agents can have their prompts edited again. [#165064](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165064) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sessions boards now hide automation, system, and Home sessions by default, including existing boards. [#165076](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165076) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/workboard) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Scheduled messages recheck permission at transport handoff and retain accepted parts when later sends fail. [#165129](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165129) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-channel-plugins) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Workboard Sessions boards avoid unnecessary reloads and repeated work for equivalent viewers while real changes still update. [#165141](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165141) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/workboard) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sessions boards show live activity, retain known PRs with outage warnings, offer PR links and display readable plain-text message previews. [#165189](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165189) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/workboard) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Full reminder queues preserve accepted reminders and explicitly fail new overflow instead of silently discarding older work. [#165305](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165305) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/troubleshooting) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/webhooks) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Scheduled turns targeting busy conversations wait for active work and final metadata updates, preventing them from invalidating in-progress history summaries. [#165310](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165310) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/payloads) · Thanks [@Linux2010](https://github.com/Linux2010), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@automaton1379](https://github.com/automaton1379), [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
- User messages queued behind heartbeats keep normal tools, context and reply delivery when execution is deferred or retried. [#165313](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165313) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Scheduled runs prepare and save session state through database workers, reducing work on the Gateway main thread while accepted writes still settle during shutdown. [#165332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165332) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Scheduled and webhook-driven Codex agents honor configured max thinking when the model supports it. [#165444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165444) Thanks [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
- Repeated automation lists and scheduler status reuse unchanged results to reduce server work. [#165629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165629) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/operator-methods) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Team Reports retains the missed day before startup when its first scheduled catch-up crosses UTC midnight. [#165780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165780) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/team-reports) · Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval).
- Automation cleanup waits for agent databases to finish startup preparation instead of reporting a premature maintenance failure. [#165814](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165814) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/managing-jobs) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Automation guides explain that manual CLI runs bypass conditions by default and show how to test eligible conditions with due mode. [#165828](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165828) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/cron) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/schedules) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@lidbai](https://github.com/lidbai).
- Doctor stops repeatedly warning about tool authority on command cron jobs that do not use agent tools. [#166384](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166384) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/payloads) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@davidchyi-beep](https://github.com/davidchyi-beep).
- Webhooks reject malformed supplied session keys and honor transform requests to wake now. [#166406](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166406) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/webhooks) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Scheduled runs keep reading their captured history when a later queued message arrives. [#166487](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166487) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@AXEG0](https://github.com/AXEG0), [@hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Repeated timezone-aware scheduling calculations reuse date formatters. [#166507](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166507) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@kzclaw](https://github.com/kzclaw).
- Scheduled command announcements save the destination conversation route without a misleading policy warning when no source session is configured. [#166836](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166836) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@jlahullier](https://github.com/jlahullier).
- Agent webhooks can wait through overlapping heartbeat database registration instead of failing before the agent starts. [#166860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166860) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Independent heartbeat work can resume after an explicitly stopped agent command finishes cleanup. [#166968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166968) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Paused child tasks retain ownership needed to continue after conversation storage changes, and expired deliveries show their original sanitized error for inspection. [#166990](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166990) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/subagents/operations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The first scheduled condition check after restart reuses loaded Gateway plugins, avoiding duplicate-loading timeouts and stalls. [#166991](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166991) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/schedules) · Thanks [@67-luck](https://github.com/67-luck), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@lucianosmori](https://github.com/lucianosmori).
- Conversations can receive delegated results and answer later messages after a queued follow-up yields to child tasks. [#167007](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167007) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/subagent-yield-handoff) · Thanks [@MoerAI](https://github.com/MoerAI), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph), [@hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Immediate background results and notifications enter their conversation's normal queue independently of periodic heartbeat settings. [#167113](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167113) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/background-process) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/heartbeat#immediate-session-events) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cron script and private-note imports reject malformed UTF-8 instead of silently altering supplied text. [#167120](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167120) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/cron) · Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Scheduled-session cleanup sends less ordinary-conversation metadata through the Gateway. [#167248](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167248) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Isolated WebChat automations return results to their creating conversation; resets or deletion can leave a destination binding that needs repair. [#167363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167363) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Subagent restart recovery avoids repeated environment setup while retaining checks against the original database. [#167372](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167372) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Removing an active scheduled job preserves its canceled status in saved session history after cleanup. [#167524](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167524) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Automation prompts collapse into expandable chat rows, with a link to the originating run in History. [#167600](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167600) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fresh scheduled sessions stop raising misleading prompt-cache drop warnings. [#167608](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167608) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Webhook retries avoid a false HTTP 502 when their first session database appears before the read completes. [#167629](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167629) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Older announcements and background tasks can proceed while newer interactive requests keep arriving. [#167683](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167683) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/queue) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@SebTardif](https://github.com/SebTardif).
- Large sub-agent task registries use less repeated CPU and temporary allocation for status and maintenance. [#167684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167684) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session cleanup continues for healthy agents while another agent is waiting for deletion cleanup. [#167907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167907) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/managing-jobs) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Temporary provider outages get their scheduled recovery retries before automations request repair or send failure alerts. [#167957](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167957) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/delivery) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/cron) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Manually started automations retain their permitted tools after the chat request that launched them ends. [#168037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168037) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/payloads) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Creating many sessions leaves room for Home reminders and notifications by combining new-session notices into one pending summary. [#168239](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168239) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/main-session) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-agents/sessions) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cron releases its current database write when host approval stalls and retries later, reducing interference with updates and other writes. [#168291](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168291) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
- Lets scheduled jobs reach their configured backup model after a primary-provider timeout without duplicating the prompt. [#168605](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168605) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/payloads) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@wongwilliam425-ui](https://github.com/wongwilliam425-ui).
- Stops unchanged one-shot automations from causing redundant Doctor recovery backups. [#168938](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168938) Thanks [@harshitgupta31415](https://github.com/harshitgupta31415), [@wg-mojo](https://github.com/wg-mojo).
- Agents get clearer guidance for returning after a wait, and eligible failed one-time automations request repair in their original conversation. [#169102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169102) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/payloads) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/delivery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@erlightbourn-source](https://github.com/erlightbourn-source), [@lonexreb](https://github.com/lonexreb).
- Eligible dashboard automation interrupted by restart draining can resume after startup instead of losing its recovery claim. [#170162](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170162) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Agent webhooks reject unavailable execution capacity so callers can retry or back off; HTTP 200 confirms admission rather than completion, and session conflicts can still return 409. [#175111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/175111) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/automation/cron-jobs/webhooks) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).



#### Installation and maintenance

Installers and updates recover through more interrupted handoffs, while Doctor and Gateway startup give clearer recovery steps when preparation fails. Scheduled workspace cleanup no longer stops unexpectedly on paired nodes.

Downgrading across SQLite schema **24 → 25** requires the matching pre-upgrade backup; restoring it loses later writes. Preserve the original data and follow Doctor's intermediate-upgrade guidance for retired state formats.

Existing Kubernetes deployments with a read-only root need a writable cache when updating the image. See the [container setup guidance](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/kubernetes#architecture-notes).



##### Installation, updates, recovery, and deployment changes


- Portable worker archives include the helper needed to open their state during startup. [22cd7d6](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/22cd7d6ab59f9fd30fd6dd87ee07590c619aa6b2)
- Older agent databases can finish Doctor migration without failing a schema check before legacy migrations complete. [343e56d](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/343e56d88714122b55b080a82515a1ca6ae0ee4b)
- Database-reader cleanup can finish despite an unreadable discovery path instead of leaving old checks retained. [357466e](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/357466e56837b934ef04dd7e54917c68f519ccb1)
- Supporting restart integration retains interrupted chat state and original CLI failures and avoids temporary password files. [383cb4b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/383cb4b0bdf71527b1cdc43567851f9d11894573)
- Node workers retain their configured memory limit even when the Gateway starts with a larger limit. [436bc7c](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/436bc7c527e3c41d51fabcc0cefe5862fd57d6aa) Thanks [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn).
- Doctor memory-wiki checks avoid loading the pairing database until a pairing read is needed. [4bac149](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/4bac1493291e7d931c0eea48b8ed07e98dc37ce0)
- The updater recognizes stable 2026.10.2 as a source for the first upgrade step. [5290333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/5290333cadab0cc9537f38ca6c50ace5d91c1438)
- Doctor maintenance refusals show the underlying database or filesystem cause. [54cfa2f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/54cfa2f8647c66fa34d72ad149a316caf8b8f6e4)
- Doctor refuses damaged older databases instead of masking missing structures during upgrade. [6785728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/678572811dd8b10c19ee69e2107947d099dd33c1) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/versioning)
- OpenClaw refuses to start work if its shared database has invalid saved ownership information, even when no writes are pending. [7cbba45](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/7cbba45c1323c89021fb50bda7c5dbaa736490ec).
- Updates recognize retained runtime dependencies through Windows short paths and case differences. [836fd79](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/836fd79faa03f154458b895be6e0e4f38d0b705c)
- Fix an unnecessary cache setup error that stopped some anonymous diagnostic requests before they could run. [8d91b9c8](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/8d91b9c85e264a80a5eb79434a6a90797489502f)
- Doctor checks tool schemas using the selected plugin generation, avoiding duplicate registrations. [965ac0f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/965ac0ffc3e8ec99a1923b48e30f27ab7391ab9b)
- Background database verification keeps its writer available until scanning and the saved verification result finish. [978478f](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/978478ff01afcb73143bc63da5f19f1218c888b7) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery)
- Explain disk-full plugin capture failures and direct users to free space and rerun Doctor. [a6496a2](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/a6496a2c5d1aee637a9c7fe0381ec2fc1ce524bb).
- New session database work can resume after configuration changes. [b798788](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b7987881046c51675166b36b97360fb540811060)
- Docker builds pass workspace lockfile validation while retaining optional-plugin selection. [b7e788b](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b7e788b23e7c41c8d78edc058ff15a885adac635)
- Automatic update repair can read configuration and proceed during an update. [b8602f4](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/b8602f4c8ba503b9e4ae7d91d3490449daaacd3a)
- Valid database writes can proceed after an earlier write fails. [c190df1](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/c190df12716a17d23a1c00eaf106044d2f2c9317)
- Completed updates avoid unnecessary failure triage or a second failure report. [d357abf](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d357abf71bedeb1a86e0c1fa73c27df1cd7b8c32)
- Database work already in progress can finish against its original storage after configuration changes. [d81f112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/d81f112cdf42bfcece8e5f84fe4d6fd3c24444b9)
- Normal SQLite worker shutdown preserves queued diagnostic file logs for troubleshooting. [f8f1c8a](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/f8f1c8af73a7b874db7c2693345e41a8a6033713) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/logging)
- Supporting diagnostics retain useful paths and original errors; the compact System widget matches the expanded CPU, memory, and ping order. [faf63c5](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/faf63c5898e353c2b98d06725b2348bd8bf989fd) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/config) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/web/control-ui/feature-reference)
- Diagnostic queries reject empty filters and limits instead of silently using defaults. [#145532](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145532) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/query#gateway-stability) · Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen), [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf), [@nocodet666](https://github.com/nocodet666).
- Blank saved-bundle selectors fail instead of choosing unintended diagnostic evidence. [#145537](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/145537) Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen), [@altaywtf](https://github.com/altaywtf).
- Blocked settings updates explain the refusal and next step while leaving current configuration unchanged. [#155427](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155427) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/configuration) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Gateway readiness waits keep their elapsed-time limits when the system clock changes. [#155799](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/155799) Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Source builds use less peak memory during runtime bundling by scheduling bundles one at a time. [#157739](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/157739) Thanks [@emes](https://github.com/emes), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Linux Gateways remain responsive when log rollover encounters a temporary directory removed by system cleanup. [#160433](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/160433) Thanks [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [@WadeOwenWatts](https://github.com/WadeOwenWatts), [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Model-catalog worker failures now show a reason in logs and remain counted after worker replacement. [#161740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161740) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/logging) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/architecture) · Thanks [@matthematics1137](https://github.com/matthematics1137), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Worker diagnostics preserve both the operation failure and the cleanup failure when both occur. [#162124](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162124) Thanks [@michaelswissa](https://github.com/michaelswissa), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Doctor can complete deferred external-plugin upgrades through their public setup entries. [#162275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162275) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-entrypoints/define-setup-plugin-entry) · Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
- Doctor preserves older ACP conversation bindings during upgrades and protects closed sessions from being revived by old metadata. [#162920](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/162920) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#acp-session-metadata) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Independent terminals on affected macOS Monterey hosts can stop or restart the Gateway despite denied launchctl inspection. [#163317](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163317) Thanks [@NovaUnboundAi](https://github.com/NovaUnboundAi), [@xXG0DLessXx](https://github.com/xXG0DLessXx), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Doctor moves default legacy Clawdbot state and configuration to canonical OpenClaw paths; ordinary runtime no longer discovers old paths. [#163376](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163376) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Deleting a workspace-free session also removes its leftover local GitHub publication records. [#163833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/163833) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Exec-approval documentation directs operators with saved legacy policy fields to Doctor repair. [#164234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164234) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#exec-approval-policy) · Thanks [@wakqasahmed](https://github.com/wakqasahmed), [@cristianoc](https://github.com/cristianoc), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Multiple-Gateway guidance includes sandbox listener ports to help avoid conflicts. [#164275](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164275) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/multiple-gateways) · Thanks [@Satoshi-Kusumoto](https://github.com/Satoshi-Kusumoto), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Doctor backups no longer fail falsely when Linux cannot report stable file creation times. [#164363](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164363) Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@StevenKrebs](https://github.com/StevenKrebs), [@kirylh](https://github.com/kirylh), [@stunt4711](https://github.com/stunt4711).
- Fish completions preserve backslashes in descriptions and avoid broken suggestions. [#164455](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164455) Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Reusable Gateway resources release completed request data, and Doctor refuses repairs when damaged indexes hide active or conflicting Gateway ownership. [#164465](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164465) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway startup errors retain the original cause even when cleanup also fails. [#164500](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164500) Thanks [@sunlit-deng](https://github.com/sunlit-deng), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Package updates can continue after full verification reaches resource limits, with a visible reduced-verification warning. [#164504](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164504) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods#alternative-manual-npm-pnpm-or-bun) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Persistent database workers on supporting Bun runtimes can reclaim released JavaScript heap while idle. [#164567](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164567) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Non-root package updates and rollback tolerate launcher ownership that the updater cannot reproduce. [#164636](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164636) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods#alternative-manual-npm-pnpm-or-bun) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hrp6vv9mwr-glitch](https://github.com/hrp6vv9mwr-glitch), [@cmillwat](https://github.com/cmillwat).
- Valid workspace avatar paths no longer fail the avatar check in disposable update validation. [#164638](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164638) Thanks [@nitinjwadhawan](https://github.com/nitinjwadhawan), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [@BottaniCals](https://github.com/BottaniCals).
- Doctor repairs stale string subagent targets without crashing on malformed allowlist entries; retired sandbox registries now require an intermediate upgrade. [#164669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164669) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Database recovery begins earlier after unclean restarts, and recovered agents remain available in chat metadata and model listings. [#164670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164670) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- SQLite inspection subprocesses inherit explicit supported Node compiler settings, and Google Meet connection failures use consistent errors. [#164675](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164675) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Affected recovery and package-install paths keep installation scripts on the selected compatible Node runtime. [#164677](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164677) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/node) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jarvisnextgeneration20-lang](https://github.com/jarvisnextgeneration20-lang).
- Windows plugin preparation avoids redundant file opens, reducing a measured source of Gateway and model-status delay. [#164680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164680) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@absol89](https://github.com/absol89).
- Doctor shows the actual database refusal or recovery information without temporary snapshot cleanup masking it. [#164697](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164697) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor can re-enable and start an owned disabled Windows Gateway task and explains its disabled state. [#164728](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164728) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/windows) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@taoxia255-del](https://github.com/taoxia255-del).
- The updater announces each validation check and command before it starts. [#164746](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164746) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor preserves and can migrate legacy audit logs when native no-replace filesystem moves are unsupported. [#164748](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164748) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery#skipped-legacy-audit-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@bbs2i58](https://github.com/bbs2i58).
- Device setup links move join-code storage work off the Gateway thread while retaining one-use exchange. [#164759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164759) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updated npm updaters can recover a Docker image-layer package rename failure using a verified rollback copy. [#164769](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164769) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods#alternative-manual-npm-pnpm-or-bun) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital).
- Slow-response logs retain recent request timings when the bounded timing cache fills. [#164772](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164772) Thanks [@etzelm](https://github.com/etzelm), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Update history and Settings can retain the actual refusal and its repair guidance. [#164782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164782) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@coygeek](https://github.com/coygeek).
- Installing completion preserves significant escaped trailing spaces in existing shell-profile commands. [#164785](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164785) Thanks [@yetval](https://github.com/yetval), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Settled agent databases can close cleanly while unrelated shutdown cleanup remains pending. [#164788](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164788) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- POSIX npm updates admit writable prefix parents owned by another user and reject unsafe package ownership earlier with useful details. [#164797](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164797) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@potterdigital](https://github.com/potterdigital).
- Interrupted-update recovery accepts identical files despite timestamp-only drift. [#164823](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164823) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible Linux Gateway workers can reopen agent sessions sooner after a verified process crash while full integrity checks continue in the background. [#164846](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164846) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery#integrity-checks) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update repair can settle obsolete recovery ownership after its database was replaced, allowing subsequent updates while retaining evidence. [#164849](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164849) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@szef-svg](https://github.com/szef-svg), [@izrl613](https://github.com/izrl613).
- Automatic session cleanup moves cold subagent-protection reads off the Gateway thread while preserving active and pending-delivery sessions. [#164851](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164851) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@todddickerson](https://github.com/todddickerson).
- Doctor drains retained workers before CLI exit, and update history preserves completed-check diagnostics even if a later crash fails the candidate. [#164853](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164853) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/running) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@cunzai007-hub](https://github.com/cunzai007-hub).
- Refused package updates identify the recovery object and unsafe metadata without exposing absolute installation paths or contents. [#164858](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164858) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/status-and-history) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xydigitLybnnnn](https://github.com/xydigitLybnnnn), [@creanet-64](https://github.com/creanet-64), [@jrpasolini](https://github.com/jrpasolini), [@jb168](https://github.com/jb168), [@burchk007-jpg](https://github.com/burchk007-jpg).
- Doctor imports sessions blocked by older migration receipts while preserving archives and existing SQLite metadata when history is uncertain. [#164867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164867) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/sqlite-maintenance) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hshs38033-cmd](https://github.com/hshs38033-cmd).
- Update repair can finish its own completed Doctor work while a separate Doctor command remains active. [#164868](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164868) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unused agent databases no longer block Gateway startup and readiness; their bytes remain intact and a warning points to Doctor. [#164929](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164929) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333).
- Session maintenance prepares remote-worker preservation facts outside the Gateway main thread and rejects stale facts before cleanup commits. [#164943](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164943) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reconnecting clients share update-status preparation instead of repeating the same notification reads, while later outcomes continue to refresh. [#164984](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164984) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/status-and-history) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor and updates can publish and roll back configuration in a writable state folder owned by another user, including Kubernetes fsGroup setups. [#164990](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164990) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/state-and-sessions) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@saftall](https://github.com/saftall).
- Update recovery retains the service identity needed after systemd unloads a stopped unit, and the repaired CLI can retire a verified preparation that never began installation. [#164997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164997) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jorden2895](https://github.com/jorden2895).
- Prepared child commands keep using the release they were prepared with when an update changes a launcher symlink before they start. [#164998](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164998) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Grynn](https://github.com/Grynn).
- Source-checkout Gateways prepare their first worker environment faster. [#165015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165015) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Scheduled automations remain available after Doctor repair without an extra restart. [#165032](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165032) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Memory warnings reflect actual runtime capacity, avoiding unnecessary retirement of warm workers. [#165042](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165042) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/diagnostics) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- macOS Gateway recovery preserves a newer runtime or service choice made by the operator. [#165044](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165044) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Busy Gateways avoid memory buildup behind slow requests. [#165052](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165052) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Source updates to locally cached pinned commits avoid unnecessary fetching and accept full SHA-256 IDs. [#165060](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165060) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Agent databases can close cleanly during busy restarts even if unrelated plugin cleanup stalls. [#165069](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165069) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible Linux crash restarts restore usable sessions sooner, and large session cleanup avoids repeated work. [#165070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165070) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cleanup of large diagnostic histories does less database work. [#165074](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165074) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes#trajectory-retention-covering-index) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Cleanup preserves temporary plugin and model-catalog files still in use or whose use cannot be checked. [#165079](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165079) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/architecture#runtime-instance-and-source-lifetime) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Dhotei](https://github.com/Dhotei).
- Session cleanup planning reads less unrelated saved data in large installations. [#165082](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165082) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Routine maintenance helps delivery-history lookups choose a more selective database index. [#165085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165085) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Rosetta installations avoid unsafe native calls during worktree creation and process checks. [#165088](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165088) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large transcript stores spend less work on status checks and stale search-index cleanup. [#165090](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165090) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Smaller agents can become available after restart while larger agents are still preparing, with recovery progress in logs. [#165107](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165107) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Nested documentation examples retain their authored indentation when displayed and copied, including Slack setup manifests. [#165127](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165127) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/slack/setup) · Thanks [@x-n2o](https://github.com/x-n2o), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Updates finish their selected run's deferred model repairs even when plugins are unchanged. [#165148](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165148) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible Windows Gateway tasks can start before login, and managed startup allows longer cold starts. [#165163](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165163) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/windows) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/restart-and-supervision) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@desksk](https://github.com/desksk), [@runvouch](https://github.com/runvouch).
- Restart recovery settles orphaned spawned and archived sessions left marked running while preserving resumable main sessions and active work. [#165174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165174) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delegated work resumes after deferred database readiness, and one failed requester group no longer blocks unrelated recovery. [#165184](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165184) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Runtime database opens avoid repeated completed integrity checks, and slow-hold warnings identify the operation and time spent waiting or committing. [#165186](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165186) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Agents with small conversation-history rebuilds can become available sooner after a multi-agent Gateway restart. [#165207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165207) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fresh installs without Matrix or legacy Matrix data avoid an unnecessary Doctor migration failure while the Gateway is running. [#165209](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165209) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Non-interactive onboarding returns requested JSON error details when affected provider setup fails, helping installers explain the failure. [#165211](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165211) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/onboard) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large Gateways can serve requests before optional startup maintenance finishes, with clearer phase timings for diagnosis. [#165218](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165218) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/logging) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- In-place updates avoid a misleading missing-module cleanup error after success, and failed commands retain their original error. [#165233](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165233) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update repair can recover the covered blockage when reboot or temporary-file cleanup removes the handoff database. [#165238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165238) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Hensarj](https://github.com/Hensarj), [@dawid-sitko](https://github.com/dawid-sitko).
- Updates to an already-current package can still repair supported legacy configuration and complete plugin maintenance without --channel. [#165242](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165242) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Retained workers exchange task messages directly, spending less CPU and time moving inputs and results. [#165249](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165249) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Old plugin-session artifact cleanup skips recent snapshots and reads historical windows in pages, reducing planning work and long read holds. [#165254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165254) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates can copy file bytes when a container blocks file cloning, avoiding the covered updater-runtime-retention failure. [#165257](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165257) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@MrJanvar](https://github.com/MrJanvar), [@rybing7](https://github.com/rybing7), [@Mohl](https://github.com/Mohl).
- Windows service reinstalls replace the old running process so runtime paths, arguments and environment changes actually take effect; status verifies the registered command. [#165261](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165261) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/windows) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Full source builds compile overlapping public declarations together instead of repeating SDK declaration work. [#165271](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165271) Thanks [@huntharo](https://github.com/huntharo), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Doctor recognizes native Claude CLI installations outside the service PATH and avoids misleading reinstall advice. [#165273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165273) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/checks) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx).
- Cleaning old diagnostic run history causes shorter interruptions to other writes in the same agent database. [#165274](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165274) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/trajectory) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A retained background-worker initialization failure stops repeated replacement starts driven by queued tasks; healthy siblings keep working. [#165277](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165277) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Busy Gateways avoid repeated process work when preparing PR information and protected HTTPS commands to the same hostname. [#165290](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165290) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/secrets/secret-store-and-egress) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session worker reads reuse unchanged ownership metadata and existing read transactions to reduce repeated database work while keeping related facts current. [#165293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165293) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Initial saved-session reads for replies and fast commands move off the Gateway thread while preserving session and save safeguards. [#165295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165295) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates preserve existing Feishu callback endpoints before activation even when plugin installation is deferred; shared fallback covers Telegram, Teams and Nextcloud Talk. [#165303](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165303) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-overview/infrastructure) · Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Update status avoids inflated ahead/behind counts in shallow Git histories, returning unknown when exact comparison cannot be justified. [#165316](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165316) Thanks [@ooiuuii](https://github.com/ooiuuii).
- Existing shared Bun consumers receive VM rendering, asynchronous import-context and worker heap/stack-limit engine fixes. [#165317](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165317) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Generated macOS service definitions request a longer shutdown window, with Doctor and update finalization migrating recognized older generated definitions while preserving custom settings. [#165320](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165320) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@yuzh086-create](https://github.com/yuzh086-create).
- An agent repairing its startup indexes promptly asks callers to retry while other ready agents, Control UI and health checks remain usable. [#165323](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165323) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates of prepared source builds and package installations skip an unnecessary plugin-state lock during runtime completion, avoiding unrelated lock admission failures at that step. [#165326](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165326) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Pending session notices are restored after restart through background database workers, keeping their recovery SQL off the Gateway main thread. [#165331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165331) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
