- Workspace recovery spends less time checking large numbers of files that are already deleted. [#165341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165341) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Dev-channel updates can prepare a source checkout before its dependencies are installed instead of failing with a missing-package error. [#165347](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165347) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor can wait for an exited container's fresh ownership lock to expire instead of immediately refusing maintenance, while protecting active Gateways. [#165355](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165355) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Queued health checks and inference requests keep waiting until their actual deadline when the clock moves backward or the deadline exceeds the native timer range. [#165359](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165359) Thanks [@Chessing234](https://github.com/Chessing234), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Configuration checks show the actual SQLite helper launch error and affected file instead of a misleading trim TypeError. [#165373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165373) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/config) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@nnljl2003](https://github.com/nnljl2003).
- File copying falls back safely when containers block cloning, and the filesystem dependency also corrects fresh file creation on gVisor/9p. [#165374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165374) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@MrJanvar](https://github.com/MrJanvar), [@Mohl](https://github.com/Mohl), [@rybing7](https://github.com/rybing7).
- Two retired configuration formats require an intermediate upgrade through 2026.9.5 and openclaw doctor --fix before installing a newer release. [#165375](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165375) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Six retired JSON state families require 2026.9.7 Doctor on the original host before upgrading; current Doctor preserves the files and explains that step. [#165402](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165402) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery#agent-requested-restarts) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed npm installs honor uppercase and mixed-case retry and timeout settings, including zero retries. [#165409](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165409) Thanks [@Chessing234](https://github.com/Chessing234).
- Agent diagnostic recorder setup uses background session readers, reducing synchronous Gateway-thread database work without changing captured diagnostics. [#165410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165410) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Authorized updates no longer fail because Doctor's permission check mistakes its own maintenance session for another owner's lock. [#165417](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165417) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unused old login files no longer block Doctor, upgrades or container startup. [#165420](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165420) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- An obsolete update-notification cache no longer blocks Doctor or upgrade admission and remains preserved. [#165461](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165461) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Sessions accept the next instruction after restart recovery settles the interrupted reply. [#165469](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165469) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Opening messages and usage reports moves their database reads off the main request thread. [#165470](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165470) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Large worker-session inventories cause less repeated work during cleanup checks, and changed placements invalidate old cleanup decisions. [#165475](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165475) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/cloud-workers/session-lifecycle) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restart inspection no longer produces false secret-failure warnings or unnecessary reload advice. [#165485](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165485) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/secrets/runtime-model) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway startup skips unnecessary repair-rule loading, and recovery inspections read a consistent state view. [#165498](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165498) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/running) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Routine session cleanup does less repeated work on large watcher registries while preserving retention safeguards. [#165511](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165511) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Usage reports reject dates skipped by the Gateway timezone instead of returning backwards ranges. [#165523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165523) Thanks [@SashaMIT](https://github.com/SashaMIT), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- An external Gateway restart can hand over after required data closes even when plugin shutdown stalls. [#165524](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165524) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor explains retired runtime files without letting advisory inspection failures block an otherwise valid startup. [#165528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165528) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows Gateway status preserves a saved Node or Bun choice after installation and gives Task Scheduler repair guidance. [#165538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165538) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/windows) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Optional configuration fallbacks during updates appear as warnings instead of misleading errors. [#165539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165539) Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Linux Gateways automatically warm bounded recent-history data in the background to reduce later disk reads. [#165545](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165545) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/layout) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prometheus separates worker queue waiting from request duration so operators can diagnose contention. [#165591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165591) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/prometheus) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update repair can unblock a stranded publication after external package changes once modified inventoried files are restored. [#165607](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165607) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@NovaeonStudio](https://github.com/NovaeonStudio).
- After restart, a long recovered turn no longer delays unrelated interrupted sessions beyond normal main-lane limits. [#165612](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165612) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway startup reuses configuration checks and plugin information to reduce repeated setup work. [#165667](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165667) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway shutdown logs identify slow cleanup steps and show what is still pending when a deadline expires. [#165670](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165670) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Old diagnostic run history can be cleaned up while new events continue arriving. [#165678](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165678) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- First-use agent database checks move off the Gateway thread, and background observer writes stop blocking turn cleanup through an inherited write context. [#165680](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165680) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large subagent histories cause fewer Gateway pauses during recovery and cold reads, and restored state is published together. [#165684](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165684) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The earlier removal of Voice Call conversion is superseded by the restored Doctor migration below; current supported older settings need not all be converted manually. [#165707](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165707) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/voice-call/configuration) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session activity follows live runs and queues so interrupted restarts do not leave stale Working indicators; local destructive cleanup now requires exclusive ownership. [#165733](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165733) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/sessions) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/layout) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Setup and troubleshooting guides describe connection checks in clearer everyday language. [#165736](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165736) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Connection buttons, results and native diagnostics use clearer check wording. [#165737](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165737) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- CLI setup, status and repair messages describe connection and authentication checks in clearer language. [#165740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165740) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Runtime errors and health messages describe existing checks more clearly. [#165742](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165742) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Linux updates respect systemd maintenance holds and show how to recover when a hold prevents restart. [#165747](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165747) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/service#linux-maintenance-holds) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@gregb100](https://github.com/gregb100).
- Database schema histories identify the releases that introduced stored-data changes, helping operators plan upgrades and recovery. [#165757](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165757) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/state-schema-history) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/agent-schema-history) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@gregb100](https://github.com/gregb100).
- Affected Linux hosts no longer reject healthy database snapshots solely because metadata timestamps changed. [#165775](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165775) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@donquiche61](https://github.com/donquiche61), [@naturecurly](https://github.com/naturecurly), [@dreamkeeper](https://github.com/dreamkeeper), [@wangmiao0668000666](https://github.com/wangmiao0668000666), [@clockthunder](https://github.com/clockthunder), [@honghongxinxi-zhb](https://github.com/honghongxinxi-zhb), [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309).
- Interrupted Gateway restarts can finish database closure when scheduled continuations are still active. [#165782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165782) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Worktree cleanup remembers invalid Git metadata instead of repeatedly probing it, and the X plugin regains its Plugins-page icon. [#165795](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165795) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Slow preparation of individual agent databases no longer holds up Gateway readiness; available agents and the Control UI can be used while the remaining agents finish. [#165803](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165803) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prometheus metrics identify the worker tasks and database operations behind queue delays. Dashboard queries using older task labels need updating. [#165816](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165816) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/prometheus) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor and update backups preserve vector memory data on CPUs that cannot load the bundled vector-search extension, protecting recovery before repairs. [#165820](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165820) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/backups) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@NovaUnboundAi](https://github.com/NovaUnboundAi).
- The experimental fleet command is retired; retained containers and data need Docker or Podman management for deployment, updates, and backups. Related transcript completions keep their recorded owner. [#165825](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165825) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating#retired-tenant-container-management) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery#downgrade) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/security/trust-model) · Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Managed CLI updates respect explicitly selected temporary directories when planning snapshots, while retaining capacity-based fallbacks. [#165830](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165830) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Doctor can again repair supported older Voice Call settings so they pass validation after an upgrade, while preserving current-format values. [#165831](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165831) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/voice-call) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor archive repairs keep progressing through empty historical session IDs and respond to interruption, preserving completed batches for retry and reporting the affected archive on failure. [#165837](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165837) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@A1fred-AI](https://github.com/A1fred-AI), [@goncalonc](https://github.com/goncalonc), [@mpquemel](https://github.com/mpquemel).
- An unfinished channel-health check no longer causes an otherwise verified update to fail solely for timing out; the update keeps a warning directing users to check health. [#165852](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165852) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vasaeru](https://github.com/vasaeru), [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna), [@rogerallen1](https://github.com/rogerallen1), [@TimofeyK](https://github.com/TimofeyK).
- Doctor and updates can resume interrupted archive publication, retain missing-copy recovery information, and restore a managed Gateway Doctor stopped when a repair fails. [#165854](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165854) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@Olli0103](https://github.com/Olli0103), [@h6y3](https://github.com/h6y3), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), [@rauf543](https://github.com/rauf543), [@mpquemel](https://github.com/mpquemel), [@goncalonc](https://github.com/goncalonc), [@A1fred-AI](https://github.com/A1fred-AI), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates from 2026.10.1-beta.1 can finish cleanup after package replacement without losing required modules. [#165855](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165855) Thanks [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Doctor can clear unfinished-plugin warnings for eligible empty configurations or a completed official replacement, while retaining plugin data and genuine migration work. [#165861](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165861) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Franklinyung](https://github.com/Franklinyung), [@ekinnee](https://github.com/ekinnee).
- Catchable interruptions during local update finalization give recovery time to restore a consistent package and restart the Gateway before reporting the signal and phase. [#165863](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165863) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@chrisreed14](https://github.com/chrisreed14), [@dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization).
- Doctor and update-time migration can import older saved pending replies without repeatedly failing, preserving exact conversation IDs, transcripts and archived originals. [#165866](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165866) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations) · Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@609NFT](https://github.com/609NFT).
- Gateway suspension can finish after already-disconnected requests or failed final dispatches instead of waiting on stale work, and reports the conversations still awaiting writes. [#165871](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165871) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/query) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The Control UI's generated assets are readable by arbitrary container users, addressing the permission failure that prevented it loading in OpenShift deployments. [#165880](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165880) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@Conan-Scott](https://github.com/Conan-Scott).
- Secret-file inputs reject unwritten named pipes, plugin logs stay out of machine output, Doctor preserves replaced links, and custom Mistral costs survive configuration cleanup. [#165900](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165900) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/secrets) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Prometheus metrics identify shared-state read families, and node pending-work drains avoid a redundant pairing lookup while retaining fresh authorization. [#165910](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165910) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/prometheus) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Background worktree cleanup releases sessions sooner and backs off repeated Git timeouts. [#165913](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165913) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Git and worktree failures retain their real diagnostic when a generated path contains dns. [#165921](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165921) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large worker fleets do less repeated background reconciliation work. [#165922](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165922) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Idle Gateways avoid unnecessary scheduler wakeups and temporary allocations. [#165931](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165931) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repeated node and environment discovery does less work when paired devices and node state are unchanged. [#165941](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165941) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateways restore large retained subagent registries faster before becoming ready. [#165942](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165942) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Worktree cleanup reports complete totals for eligible removals, deferrals and failures even beyond its detail limit. [#165969](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165969) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed worktree Git indexes can share unchanged file data on APFS and other clone-capable filesystems. [#165981](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165981) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@matthematics1137](https://github.com/matthematics1137).
- Updates accept unchanged plugin files after harmless hard-link metadata changes while still checking their contents. [#165982](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165982) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@wg-mojo](https://github.com/wg-mojo).
- Managed-worktree maintenance recovers when a stale Git pack index refers to replaced pack files. [#165995](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165995) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Global npm installs avoid retaining native binaries for unrelated platforms, reducing installation size. [#165997](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165997) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@tommyfive](https://github.com/tommyfive), [@kenmege](https://github.com/kenmege).
- Gateway stops can finish accepted storage writes and close databases cleanly after active-work draining times out. [#166018](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166018) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Routine chat history checks and session saves perform fewer database operations. [#166041](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166041) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor can migrate legacy state on Linux systems such as QNAP where no-replace rename is unsupported. [#166048](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166048) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery#skipped-legacy-audit-recovery) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/security/secure-file-operations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@StarGazer1995](https://github.com/StarGazer1995), [@bbs2i58](https://github.com/bbs2i58), [@MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0).
- Chat preparation and worker recovery avoid repeated local database reads. [#166053](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166053) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Ongoing chats and history reads reuse unchanged session information instead of repeating database queries. [#166054](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166054) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows installs and upgrades accept equivalent short and long session-database paths instead of refusing startup. [#166062](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166062) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Session-history reader workers retain less memory by loading fewer unrelated dependencies. [#166069](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166069) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Credential-free Hermes migration plans can proceed despite an unreadable destination credential store. [#166070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166070) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed-worktree cleanup avoids parsing entire saved conversations to check whether checkouts are still in use. [#166072](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166072) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat turns and session history reads perform less database bookkeeping while preserving consistent snapshots. [#166074](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166074) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed transcript archive writes clean up their own temporary files, including storage-full failures. [#166085](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166085) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Agent operations avoid rechecking unchanged database catalog information. [#166102](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166102) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Busy Bun Gateways avoid expensive detailed heap checks between database and history operations. [#166104](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166104) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway startup waits through temporary database schema ownership instead of exiting before channels come online. [#166111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166111) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/running) · Thanks [@NianJiuZst](https://github.com/NianJiuZst), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ericcaiwx-star](https://github.com/ericcaiwx-star), [@mashaohua1999-stack](https://github.com/mashaohua1999-stack).
- Completed background full database checks can be reused after clean shutdown, avoiding a repeated scan on the next eligible restart. [#166112](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166112) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Future updater runs avoid redundant database snapshots during readiness checks. [#166126](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166126) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Account refresh and personal model discovery move database work away from the Gateway request thread and reduce repeated reads. [#166183](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166183) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Git-operation diagnostics release data retained after completed operations. [#166214](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166214) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New exec and stdio MCP launches can find an available stable Node path after Homebrew removes the old executable. [#166222](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166222) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/node) · Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx).
- Linux updates handle older stopped-service handoffs when systemd has unloaded the unit and no manager ID was recorded. [#166232](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166232) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Setup and repair helpers give failure-specific recovery advice instead of recommending onboarding for temporary runtime failures. [#166234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166234) Thanks [@PollyBot13](https://github.com/PollyBot13), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Colton-Harris](https://github.com/Colton-Harris), [@tyler194653](https://github.com/tyler194653), [@stemkat100](https://github.com/stemkat100), [@MaikiMolto](https://github.com/MaikiMolto), [@explainanalyze](https://github.com/explainanalyze), [@edith-vitaetpax](https://github.com/edith-vitaetpax), [@spiral-cmd](https://github.com/spiral-cmd), [@FtlC-ian](https://github.com/FtlC-ian), [@jj5638521](https://github.com/jj5638521), [@iamade](https://github.com/iamade), [@dh-js](https://github.com/dh-js), [@JoPaMu](https://github.com/JoPaMu), [@skorfmann](https://github.com/skorfmann), [@ryanography](https://github.com/ryanography).
- Crash-loop safe mode pauses automatic session replay, preserves interrupted work, and schedules resumption after the stable window clears. [#166238](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166238) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery#safety-valves-and-observability) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino), [@KoalaShen](https://github.com/KoalaShen).
- Restart readiness inspects shared-state databases outside the Gateway request thread while retaining refusal checks. [#166252](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166252) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Ongoing chats avoid repeated database preparation when validated structure remains unchanged. [#166253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166253) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Conversation discovery and session associations batch database work through existing workers. [#166259](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166259) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-runtime/channel) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Active SQLite paths reduce redundant retention, append and initialization work. [#166265](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166265) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Older node hosts fail worker admission early with an update-required explanation instead of rejecting new prompt context during launch. [#166268](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166268) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Profile and recent-project requests prepare data in workers and avoid repeated lookups. [#166283](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166283) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- History and file reads move database work off the Gateway thread. [#166293](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166293) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Updates prepare private validation state faster for large plugin installations while retaining existing checks. [#166317](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166317) Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
- Doctor and migration maintenance avoid redundant blocking database reads. [#166331](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166331) Thanks [@sasan1200](https://github.com/sasan1200), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unrelated system processes with unreadable working directories stop blocking cleanup of unused temporary runtimes. [#166335](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166335) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Doctor tool inspection reuses plugin registrations and owns provider discovery cleanup consistently. [#166364](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166364) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Enabled journal diagnostics explain where slow Git reads spend time without logging repository content. [#166381](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166381) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/logging) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible completed Linux update receipts can reconcile changed device numbers so a later update can proceed. [#166394](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166394) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@neilofneils404](https://github.com/neilofneils404).
- Doctor can reconcile leftover session files without replacing current sessions or stopping unrelated repairs. [#166397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166397) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/sqlite-maintenance) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@coolcoolwcr](https://github.com/coolcoolwcr).
- Failed update checks explain the requirement and identify the selected installation and runtime. [#166410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166410) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery#before-you-upgrade) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@TOM-WANG-GUANG](https://github.com/TOM-WANG-GUANG).
- Background integrity checks retain successful verification through idle worker retirement for reuse after a clean restart. [#166412](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166412) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Pre-repair captures can publish through an exclusive fallback when native no-replace rename is unsupported. [#166425](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166425) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0).
- Exec and stdio MCP relays recover after Homebrew removes the running Node, with restart guidance for exact-runtime workers. [#166430](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166430) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/status) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/node) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mightyx](https://github.com/mightyx).
- Session moves and approval preparation do more database work in the background, and deletion can finish when another cleanup has already retired the same placement. [#166444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166444) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Opening session file lists, downloading files, and viewing members avoids repeated database work while preserving current access checks. [#166447](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166447) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway error logs identify the session and reason when a requested wake fails. [#166458](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166458) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hawkit](https://github.com/hawkit).
- macOS worker failures clean up background commands and release worker capacity without a stalled process lookup. [#166478](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166478) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Interrupted Doctor recovery captures remain available and no longer hide nearby healthy captures. [#166495](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166495) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery#original-state-captures) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@MertBasar0](https://github.com/MertBasar0), [@hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Repeated session history and search work avoids unnecessary database reopening and validation. [#166503](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166503) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hippoley](https://github.com/hippoley).
- Memory reindex locks no longer cause false held-database warnings in Doctor. [#166517](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166517) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@noelff](https://github.com/noelff).
- Chat turns avoid rereading session details already known after saving. [#166519](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166519) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Concurrent Gateway CLI calls load credentials with less overhead and stop clearly if required device identity cannot be read. [#166523](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166523) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/query) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Rescue approvals and OpenRouter catalog storage move their database operations off the Gateway thread. [#166528](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166528) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Windows update candidates can pass valid live handoffs despite changed launcher ancestry. [#166530](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166530) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@wqj11a](https://github.com/wqj11a), [@H0Nm4M1suZu](https://github.com/H0Nm4M1suZu).
- Cold device identity preparation uses the shared worker, and idle plugin cleanup skips unnecessary database work. [#166539](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166539) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Failed managed updates retain actionable handoff reasons and late diagnostic details. [#166540](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166540) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/update-troubleshooting) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@dondonjang](https://github.com/dondonjang), [@dahuangForToby](https://github.com/dahuangForToby).
- Creating sessions in large catalogs avoids repeated full scans when worker readers reopen. [#166541](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166541) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An already-current update can safely restart the Gateway after refused Doctor maintenance stopped it. [#166548](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166548) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Shirleyyyhk](https://github.com/Shirleyyyhk).
- Multiline terminal table cells no longer cause unnecessary wrapping in neighboring columns. [#166549](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166549) Thanks [@ly85206559](https://github.com/ly85206559), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Git update previews explain unavailable local target data instead of misleading remote-access advice. [#166577](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166577) Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Allows plugin repair through pending migrations and official registry lag while preserving activation safeguards. [#166588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166588) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@CodeForcer](https://github.com/CodeForcer).
- Reduces redundant copying when session updates are published to clients. [#166591](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166591) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Restores Memory and Skills worker startup for source installations whose workspace is outside the checkout. [#166606](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166606) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Lets the Gateway serve requests before saving rebuilt plugin metadata when the registry is missing or stale. [#166617](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166617) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces startup work for one-shot Gateway calls and preserves credential resolution and port-cleanup errors. [#166657](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166657) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Lets Doctor settle completed historical Codex tasks whose results cannot be delivered to their original requester, allowing otherwise-complete migrations to finish. [#166668](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166668) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#native-codex-recovery-after-tasks-removal) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@dwonshin](https://github.com/dwonshin), [@Torsie](https://github.com/Torsie), [@Hensarj](https://github.com/Hensarj), [@HMKR2025](https://github.com/HMKR2025), [@flawedhumanoid-sudo](https://github.com/flawedhumanoid-sudo), [@BsnizND](https://github.com/BsnizND), [@gisk0](https://github.com/gisk0), [@cortezhub](https://github.com/cortezhub), [@KoennaOpenclaw](https://github.com/KoennaOpenclaw), [@juanbamorelli-source](https://github.com/juanbamorelli-source).
- Prevents device-identity inspection from changing SQLite files and invalidating Doctor migration plans. [#166671](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166671) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces chat-turn database bookkeeping and keeps failure cleanup tied to the accepted session. [#166674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166674) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Checks Windows Gateway task access before staging updates and bounds Task Scheduler waits with actionable errors. [#166695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166695) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xiaozishan](https://github.com/xiaozishan), [@oxen-horse](https://github.com/oxen-horse).
- Avoids repeating database validation after installing session tracking's known temporary objects. [#166698](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166698) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces CPU work when masking secrets in large contexts and logs while preserving masking behavior. [#166701](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166701) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Reduces repeated session database work during replies and keeps accepted reads valid through legitimate compaction. [#166703](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166703) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- One-shot Gateway calls spend less time loading unused dependencies before making their request. [#166722](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166722) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large update inventories can use parallel hashing when host resources allow, while constrained hosts avoid the worker cost. [#166741](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166741) Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Configuration reads correctly identify the optional root $schema key as valid but unset. [#166745](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166745) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/config) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/configuration) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313).
- Windows alternate spellings of the same state database share one connection and lifecycle identity, addressing an alias-related access conflict. [#166755](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166755) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@taihartman](https://github.com/taihartman).
- Chat turns avoid repeated conversation-history preparation and delivery checks while preserving fresh saved-history validation. [#166804](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166804) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New-session initialization avoids waiting behind unrelated archival work for its final session count. [#166822](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166822) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Configuration reads explain that missing version and migration metadata is managed automatically instead of suggesting writes that fail. [#166830](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166830) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/config) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@sxh313](https://github.com/sxh313).
- Preserved old external-agent records without current session bindings no longer prevent Gateway startup after an update. [#166839](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166839) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · Thanks [@VACInc](https://github.com/VACInc).
- Delegated update repair turns avoid spending their preparation allowance on repeated expensive permission snapshots. [#166847](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166847) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor JSON and lint inspections leave existing SQLite databases and support files unchanged while reporting pending upgrades. [#166857](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166857) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/lint) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Source-checkout Gateway status works when prepared input contents still match despite Git marking them modified. [#166864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166864) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/query) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- The first reply after Gateway restart can start when healthy database connections open concurrently. [#166867](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166867) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible new chats use fewer storage commits to start their first turn, reducing work during bursts of session creation. [#166891](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166891) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Older builds give compatible-build or pre-update-backup guidance when newer database state also has an unreadable catalog. [#166906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166906) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/versioning) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@shakkernerd](https://github.com/shakkernerd).
- Administrators can capture CPU profiles, allocation profiles and heap snapshots on OpenClaw's supported Bun runtime. [#166933](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166933) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/diagnostics) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/troubleshooting/gateway-service-and-process) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway start waits for an already-running Gateway to become ready and reports meaningful recovery guidance without restarting it. [#166942](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166942) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/service) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/restart-and-supervision) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@odrobnik](https://github.com/odrobnik).
- Gateway diagnostics keep recognized startup failures visible after cleanup messages and direct unavailable services to useful checks. [#166946](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166946) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/service) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@odrobnik](https://github.com/odrobnik).
- Doctor warns early when configured local Tailscale Serve or Funnel cannot start because its backend needs attention. [#166947](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166947) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/tailscale) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@odrobnik](https://github.com/odrobnik).
- Interactive Doctor asks to pause the managed Gateway during separately approved repairs and restores its prior running or stopped state. [#166948](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166948) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/running) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/gateway-and-services) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@odrobnik](https://github.com/odrobnik).
- Doctor previews the actual Gateway service repair before approval and identifies warnings that still require separate action. [#166950](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166950) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/gateway-and-services) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Inspecting newer database state preserves its files and gives compatible-build or matching-backup guidance across more diagnostic commands. [#166963](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166963) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Chat and maintenance perform fewer repeated database checks while retaining existing storage and permission rules. [#166966](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166966) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Successful background database checks survive the startup timing race and clean shutdown, avoiding repeated full scans on the next open. [#166972](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166972) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Normal Gateway shutdown avoids repeated closed-session-observer warnings while chat handling and final session writes finish. [#166978](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166978) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor preserves the original agent for ownerless scheduled jobs when upgrading older multi-agent configurations. [#166996](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166996) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/state-and-sessions) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Fresh database workers and maintenance checks avoid rebuilding expected database layouts already prepared by the Gateway. [#167001](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167001) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Large saved subagent histories use less CPU and allocate less memory during maintenance and first session-list reads. [#167010](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167010) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Background worktree cleanup causes fewer long pauses, and Git housekeeping no longer downloads missing objects automatically. [#167030](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167030) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Linux Gateway stops allow more time for service-manager inspection, retry one inspection timeout and identify the stalled check. [#167040](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167040) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
- Overlapping update and Doctor commands can wait through brief coordination locks instead of failing immediately. [#167051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167051) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Manual session cleanup and transcript trimming keep more database work off the Gateway main thread. [#167070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167070) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Connecting with a target-file path preserves boundary spaces and consumes the intended file instead of its similarly named neighbor. [#167116](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167116) Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Doctor inspection can let Node finish closing native resources instead of forcing process exit. [#167125](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167125) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Cooperative Gateway suspension status can wait briefly for protected writes to finish before reporting deployment blockers. [#167184](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167184) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Plugin property updates and session discovery avoid redundant allocation and repeated path resolution. [#167235](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167235) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway housekeeping skips redundant work when pairing notifications, queued recovery and worker-maintenance state are empty or unchanged. [#167268](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167268) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Larger Gateway hosts keep fewer general-purpose reader workers, reducing potential retained worker memory. [#167295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167295) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Verified completed package updates can be cleared with update repair without discarding the retained package or recovery evidence. [#167302](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167302) Thanks [@shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman), [@dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization), [@fermentolactico](https://github.com/fermentolactico), [@chrisreed14](https://github.com/chrisreed14), [@catnipluss](https://github.com/catnipluss), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Repeated chat and scheduled-session cleanup releases unused agent lookup state that previously accumulated in long-running Gateways. [#167308](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167308) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/diagnostics) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Idle checkout cleanup preserves affected work and pauses repeated failures when a shared Git repository has missing or corrupt objects. [#167323](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167323) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Completed package recovery no longer permanently blocks later updates after verified cleanup failures, filesystem changes or replaced temporary ownership records. [#167330](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167330) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman), [@dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization), [@neilofneils404](https://github.com/neilofneils404), [@fermentolactico](https://github.com/fermentolactico), [@catnipluss](https://github.com/catnipluss), [@chrisreed14](https://github.com/chrisreed14), [@GitHoubi](https://github.com/GitHoubi).
- Diagnostic logs distinguish long-held database reservations from writes waiting behind them and preserve session timing details. [#167332](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167332) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Deep Gateway status correctly identifies an unreadable state database, exits with failure and directs operators to verified-backup recovery. [#167352](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167352) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Validated completed recovery history and obsolete-package cleanup stop blocking the next update while unfinished restoration remains protected. [#167358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167358) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Doctor can recover scheduled agent work blocked by orphaned session windows while preserving the original database in a backup. [#167366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167366) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@changjunfeng8-hue](https://github.com/changjunfeng8-hue).
- Background managed-checkout cleanup continues through unrelated sidebar, logging and model settings changes. [#167367](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167367) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Backup verification rejects corrupted manifest text instead of reporting success with silently altered metadata. [#167389](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167389) Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Connect preserves its one-shot target file when malformed text encoding prevents the file from being read. [#167399](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167399) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/connect) · Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- PowerShell completes quoted option values with valid choices instead of local-file suggestions. [#167421](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167421) Thanks [@LinzeShi](https://github.com/LinzeShi).
- New managed checkouts use the fetched or cached remote default instead of silently substituting older local code when fetching fails. [#167426](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167426) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Node identity inspection returns a parseable JSON failure and recovery hint when no identity exists. [#167437](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167437) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli#json-failures) · Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Agent Git commands avoid automatic maintenance loops that could stall work in partially cloned repositories. [#167443](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167443) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Overlapping storage measurements for independent session stores can finish sooner on hosts with spare CPU capacity. [#167449](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167449) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update checks avoid redundant temporary database copies and give clearer guidance when snapshot space runs out. [#167451](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167451) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@iFadi](https://github.com/iFadi), [@dankarization](https://github.com/dankarization), [@azuretek](https://github.com/azuretek), [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
- Managed worktree maintenance consolidates accumulated small Git packs, with cautious cleanup of abandoned temporary packs on Linux. [#167453](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167453) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor, updates and message delivery avoid premature interruption when clock differences create unusually long expiry delays. [#167467](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167467) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed projects and worktrees can recover fetches blocked by missing Git commit objects while preserving local work. [#167473](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167473) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Detailed update summaries, saved reports and human status output retain complete recorded warnings and recovery paths. [#167509](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167509) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/status-and-history) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@GitHoubi](https://github.com/GitHoubi).
- Interrupted Gateway starts clean up their own Tailscale claim so it does not block the next start. [#167514](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167514) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/tailscale) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@pandysp](https://github.com/pandysp).
- Doctor and Gateway startup reduce repeated directory checks while capturing plugin files. [#167516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167516) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@palewire](https://github.com/palewire).
- Triage can open an interactive repair conversation in an installed, signed-in Google Antigravity CLI using --agent agy. [#167530](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167530) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/triage) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@guarismo](https://github.com/guarismo).
- Doctor reuses verified, unchanged plugin source files across repair phases, reducing repeated copying during maintenance. [#167535](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167535) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/architecture) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@palewire](https://github.com/palewire).
- An old import record for another database no longer blocks Doctor from recovering available conversation files or allowing the normal update restart path. [#167538](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167538) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/sqlite-maintenance) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@bipolar84](https://github.com/bipolar84).
- Removing agents or Claws shifts database work off the Gateway main thread while preserving shared history. [#167547](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167547) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/storage-changes) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Skill learning, sandbox operations, checkout cleanup and discussions repeat less database work while checking current permissions before acting. [#167571](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167571) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway restarts avoid reusing an already-consumed request, and accepted update-failure reports can finish before shutdown. [#167572](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167572) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- An agent store awaiting preparation no longer prevents the Gateway from accepting connections. [#167627](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167627) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unrelated paused Git operations no longer block an eligible local default branch from advancing during managed-worktree creation. [#167631](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167631) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Renaming, archiving, and changing session models spend less time waiting behind stale restart recovery. [#167643](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167643) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update repair can clear a pending recovery record when the intended candidate is already installed. [#167648](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167648) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@cbhawthorne84](https://github.com/cbhawthorne84).
- Failed updates avoid the unnecessary drain wait before restoring an installation when the Gateway is confirmed stopped. [#167649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167649) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@hannnnn-l](https://github.com/hannnnn-l), [@alkor2000](https://github.com/alkor2000).
- Authentication-profile migration warnings name the cause and direct operators to Doctor repair. [#167650](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167650) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/secrets/operations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@samson1357924](https://github.com/samson1357924), [@vegisearchteam](https://github.com/vegisearchteam).
- Doctor archives removed accounts' old sender lists and continues later repairs without importing their permissions elsewhere. [#167651](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167651) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@oshunter](https://github.com/oshunter).
- Queued heartbeats no longer hold idle Gateway shutdown open, and deadline logs name pending owners. [#167666](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167666) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/gateway/restart-and-supervision) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Healthy Bun processes avoid false shared-state ownership expiry when a renewal worker starts. [#167669](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167669) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/gateway-lock) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Session and model reads just after restart wait briefly for the selected agent's database instead of immediately failing. [#167674](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167674) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/health) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Simultaneous managed worktree creation avoids branch-registration conflicts and shares remote discovery and fetching. [#167691](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167691) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed worktree removal avoids repeating shared repository maintenance for each checkout. [#167695](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167695) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor clears obsolete inspection warnings for plugins with no migration contract; a declared-empty contract with missing modules can still be misclassified. [#167699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167699) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ruicozibon](https://github.com/ruicozibon).
- Doctor verifies a restart only after the Gateway answers health checks, and gives diagnostic steps when a running service remains unresponsive. [#167713](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167713) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@CamilleValley](https://github.com/CamilleValley).
- Deferred update repairs are saved as pending and show the service-owner stop-and-rerun action needed to finish maintenance. [#167714](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167714) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ruicozibon](https://github.com/ruicozibon).
- Android and Termux accept their existing state database after ordinary SQLite writes instead of mistaking it for a replaced file. [#167719](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167719) Thanks [@yxshee](https://github.com/yxshee), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@grabowskidaniel359-design](https://github.com/grabowskidaniel359-design).
- Gateway status and Doctor clear temporary startup-preparation warnings once agents recover, avoiding unnecessary repair advice. [#167723](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167723) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Normal lease retries stop producing misleading SQLite lock-failure warnings during healthy Gateway starts. [#167754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167754) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates with --no-restart report blocked state checks as unverified readiness and explain the follow-up restart and verification steps. [#167760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167760) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ruicozibon](https://github.com/ruicozibon).
- Triage detects pending package recovery even when Doctor and update history look healthy, and directs operators to update repair. [#167782](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167782) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery#update-repair) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault).
- Update refusal messages identify the changed coordination database and point to profile-aware repair. [#167794](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167794) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), [@masterdevill](https://github.com/masterdevill).
- Update reports explain which updater ran safety checks and clarify the permanent consequences of retiring recovery originals. [#167802](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167802) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating#retire-update-recovery-data) · Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen).
- Post-install Doctor can run its commands when an older updater omits the run ID, avoiding misleading admission failures. [#167833](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167833) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@GoDiao](https://github.com/GoDiao).
- Agent-management requests report success after the saved agent changes are usable by the running gateway. [#167894](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167894) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/configuration/hot-reload) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Expired worktree recovery references are removed together, keeping them available for retry when cleanup fails. [#167899](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167899) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- New pnpm gateway launchers follow the active package after replacement instead of pointing at a deleted version. [#167905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167905) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/platforms/windows) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@SMF12138](https://github.com/SMF12138).
- Saving chats avoids repeated database validation work while rejecting malformed session records before writing. [#167932](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167932) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/agent-schema-history#canonical-writer-validation) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/versioning#schema-bumps-and-older-updaters) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Structured logs use less temporary memory while applying the same secret-masking rules. [#167943](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167943) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Parallel managed worktrees from partial clones avoid repeated scans and prepare missing files before advancing local main. [#167944](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167944) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Eligible large agent databases can open sooner while full integrity checks continue in the background. [#168008](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168008) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update repair can clear eligible abandoned updater records that kept later updates blocked after a manual installation change. [#168043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168043) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@farisoftfry](https://github.com/farisoftfry).
- Declining Doctor maintenance still shows read-only findings in the same command without applying repairs. [#168065](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168065) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/running) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/gateway-and-services) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Doctor and update repair avoid repeated integrity scans when an existing repair record needs no change. [#168069](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168069) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Tosko4](https://github.com/Tosko4), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Updates preserve a local plugin's settings and enablement when an unsafe installation path temporarily blocks discovery. [#168111](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168111) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Slow local database checks no longer cause false service-inspection timeouts during Linux Doctor and update repair. [#168161](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168161) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/recovery) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@Tosko4](https://github.com/Tosko4).
- Update repair can finish beside an active updater without disturbing that updater's ownership. [#168197](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168197) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@farisoftfry](https://github.com/farisoftfry).
- A busy state database no longer makes optional update progress logging abort the update. [#168210](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168210) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@jlapenna](https://github.com/jlapenna).
- Gateway stops that exit the process avoid waiting for each native skills watcher to close individually, addressing a macOS cleanup stall. [#168273](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168273) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Windows plugin preparation uses the existing bounded copy path for small ordinary files, addressing long Gateway startup stalls. [#168313](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168313) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Lets users unblock older updaters by safely archiving an already-completed update receipt with an independent helper. [#168388](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168388) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating#recover-a-completed-receipt-with-an-older-updater) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@k94118](https://github.com/k94118), [@kimjinsuhh](https://github.com/kimjinsuhh), [@GoldenrodScript](https://github.com/GoldenrodScript), [@neilofneils404](https://github.com/neilofneils404).
- Prevents temporary progress-output congestion from aborting update metadata inspection. [#168493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168493) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [@creanet-64](https://github.com/creanet-64).
- Lets commands and Gateway shutdown finish owned work and cleanup while restoring Node compiler defaults. [#168587](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168587) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/node-compatibility) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Offers a ready-to-copy device enrollment command for a background session host and correct hosting steps for paired devices. [#168623](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168623) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/connect) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/session-hosting) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Supplies compatibility modules needed for cleanup when updating from stable 2026.10.1. [#168740](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168740) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Preserves pending cleanup before shutdown during upgrades from published stable 2026.10.1. [#168883](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168883) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Preserves configured heap controls in captured service and installation-child update environments. [#168889](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168889) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@bluet](https://github.com/bluet).
- Points failed updates after data migration to the existing repair command with clearer recovery instructions. [#168905](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168905) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery#update-repair) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ezimerman](https://github.com/ezimerman).
- Allows clean Gateway restarts when stored cloud workers are disconnected and defers their cleanup. [#168907](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168907) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoids copying unrelated dependencies and failures caused by those files changing during updates. [#168927](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168927) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Detects known incompatible Codex plugins early and gives useful plugin-update recovery instructions. [#168930](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168930) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/plugins/uninstall-and-update) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ezimerman](https://github.com/ezimerman), [@adlbas26](https://github.com/adlbas26).
- Reduces repeated file reads during package updates. [#168958](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168958) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Avoids false Windows update rollbacks when the same recovery folder has a short-name or case-variant path. [#168968](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168968) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Reports accepted background Gateway updates as pending rather than failed. [#168993](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168993) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run#restart-handoff) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sayitfast](https://github.com/sayitfast).
- Clears stale completed-plugin repair warnings and keeps package-verification deadlines steady across clock changes. [#169004](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169004) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@adenzhou1350](https://github.com/adenzhou1350), [@WG-Mojo](https://github.com/WG-Mojo).
- Settles certain blocked 2026.9.8 recovery records and reports failure honestly while older recovery remains pending. [#169027](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169027) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@chedong](https://github.com/chedong).
- Windows upgrades from 2026.10.1 can complete the plugin migration request expected by the older updater. [#169031](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169031) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Updates preserve existing webhook callback settings when an official plugin migration is deferred. [#169035](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169035) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations#channel-webhook-listeners) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sirius1024](https://github.com/sirius1024).
- Verified successful updates retain success when result publication fails, and repair reports stopped channels with recovery guidance. [#169037](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169037) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@kvnloo](https://github.com/kvnloo), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Eligible stale update recovery records can settle automatically once the live installation is verified.
