[#169038](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169038) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating#package-publication-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mkdelta221](https://github.com/mkdelta221), [@waynegault](https://github.com/waynegault), [@clawdgawd](https://github.com/clawdgawd), [@yoyo837](https://github.com/yoyo837), [@lishewen](https://github.com/lishewen), [@nukk-pain](https://github.com/nukk-pain), [@jullianatolentino](https://github.com/jullianatolentino), [@joshpetras](https://github.com/joshpetras), [@creanet-64](https://github.com/creanet-64).
- Confirmed pacman-managed installations receive package-manager update guidance instead of self-update. [#169055](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169055) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sahilsatralkar](https://github.com/sahilsatralkar), [@Pegorim](https://github.com/Pegorim).
- Clock corrections no longer distort the recovery timeout after an interrupted update. [#169168](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169168) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) · Thanks [@SunnyShu0925](https://github.com/SunnyShu0925).
- Incomplete update recovery captures identify affected databases and relevant backup warnings. [#169282](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169282) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery#original-state-captures) · Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Update-history approval reduces blocking Gateway writes; the later host-approval handoff fix also releases its writer while permission is pending. [#169283](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169283) Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Update warnings explain why an optional candidate configuration read failed while preserving the existing service definition. [#169284](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169284) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Updates recognize alternate installation path spellings and keep workers on their original runtime, with abandoned-runtime cleanup restored. [#169292](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169292) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Doctor distinguishes unknown plugin migration backup coverage from bundled migrations already covered by core, with useful next steps. [#169295](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169295) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/state-migrations#plugin-migration-recovery-coverage) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-migration/compatibility-policy) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Windows SQLite recovery no longer rejects its own protected temporary snapshot directory. [#169397](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169397) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A separately installed helper can clear a narrowly eligible unfinished publication after verified filesystem device drift while preserving package and evidence. [#169410](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169410) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating#recover-an-unfinished-publication-after-a-remount) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@patrickschiller](https://github.com/patrickschiller), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- New recovery copies preserve legacy Canvas documents and Memory Core files before migrations change them. [#169436](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169436) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Git updates clean owned temporary worktrees even when progress reporting fails and retain the original failure for diagnosis and recovery. [#169454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169454) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph).
- Equivalent Windows task account names no longer prevent the Gateway restarting after an update. [#169481](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169481) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update and Doctor checks accept explicitly empty agent rosters and reject unsafe Canvas folder links before reading them. [#169516](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169516) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Npm-global updates avoid scanning unrelated global tools while retaining dependencies OpenClaw actually needs. [#169551](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169551) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen).
- Doctor can continue an update after a later process-inspection failure once its owning updater was already verified. [#169563](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169563) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Updates can restart a revalidated preserved Gateway service after refresh safely refuses to rewrite its definition. [#169658](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169658) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update-time Doctor can verify its updater without inspecting higher supervisors and retains command identity when binding fails. [#169665](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169665) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Doctor imports deferred ACP metadata from verified archived legacy sessions once its plugin becomes available. [#169759](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169759) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Doctor can attempt Gateway restoration after transient resource-close errors settle successfully on retry. [#169783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169783) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Mac updates avoid self-lock timeouts when temporary directories have equivalent path spellings. [#169860](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169860) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Older Node updaters retain valid ownership across the heartbeat clock transition instead of unnecessarily rolling back. [#169915](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169915) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- New or repaired pnpm Gateway service commands survive package replacement, and nested pnpm installs are recognized for updates. [#170003](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170003) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@xilopaint](https://github.com/xilopaint), [@k-sekula](https://github.com/k-sekula).
- Background database checkpoints pause during retained offline maintenance to keep Doctor and update recovery captures stable. [#170022](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170022) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Configured agents can start despite bogus legacy sentinel registrations, and standalone Doctor can clean invalid registrations. [#170043](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170043) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/state-and-sessions) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ourcoms2018](https://github.com/ourcoms2018).
- Update repair can reconcile an interrupted installation after filesystem device numbers change and unblock later updates. [#170046](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170046) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@patrickschiller](https://github.com/patrickschiller), [@SergeiAug](https://github.com/SergeiAug).
- Doctor rejects unsupported legacy runtime files before changing the database or stopping the Gateway, preserving the intermediate-upgrade path. [#170051](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170051) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/config-migrations) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@bautrey](https://github.com/bautrey).
- Updates requested through chat can finish Doctor cleanup without a false maintenance-permission failure. [#170070](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170070) Thanks [@hannesrudolph](https://github.com/hannesrudolph), [@ezimerman](https://github.com/ezimerman), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@yoyo837](https://github.com/yoyo837), [@aia007731-lab](https://github.com/aia007731-lab), [@justinforsound](https://github.com/justinforsound).
- Updates avoid reopening migrated data during configuration saves and can report a recorded schema migration as advisory; same-version wrong-build concerns remain unresolved. [#170130](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170130) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@angeliti999](https://github.com/angeliti999).
- Native pnpm 12 updates keep the openclaw command pointing at the installed package after temporary files are removed. [#170131](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170131) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@goutamadwant](https://github.com/goutamadwant), [@k-sekula](https://github.com/k-sekula).
- Doctor can find abandoned npm updater runtimes that leave later updates blocked by an extra recovery-database link. [#170170](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170170) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sunnyshu0925](https://github.com/sunnyshu0925), [@warhangel](https://github.com/warhangel).
- Managed updates use per-user temporary storage and can recover eligible receipts that point into another user's directory. [#170174](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170174) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/repair-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@holgergruenhagen](https://github.com/holgergruenhagen).
- Older updaters stop rejecting a validated candidate solely because Doctor changed version or wizard bookkeeping. [#170195](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170195) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@maestrokling](https://github.com/maestrokling), [@frxiaobei](https://github.com/frxiaobei).
- Failed-update reports keep Doctor recovery instructions when a healthy Gateway does not prove Doctor writers have safely finished. [#170207](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170207) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update repair loads the target installation's settings before choosing a model instead of stopping with a configuration-not-prepared error. [#170276](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170276) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateways with deferred database preparation can finish startup instead of leaving worker recovery and client readiness waiting on each other. [#170348](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170348) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/integrity-and-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update snapshot capture stops falsely treating an unchanged agent database as changed by its own read. [#170351](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170351) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/rollback-and-recovery) · Thanks [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Large configurations can reach the updater intact instead of causing candidate-read failures during Gateway service refresh. [#170373](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170373) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Slow update snapshots stop consuming the next command's inspection time, and genuinely deferred checks get follow-up guidance. [#170374](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170374) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Final update summaries and saved history stop calling completed plugin upgrades unfinished and retain actionable warnings for pending ones. [#170413](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170413) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/status-and-history) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Stalled transcript indexing gets a five-second grace then cancellation so it does not indefinitely block Gateway shutdown. [#170493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170493) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/restart-recovery) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Oldrich333](https://github.com/Oldrich333), [@O96a](https://github.com/O96a).
- Update and Doctor recovery can continue when optional dreaming schedule checks or settled temporary-snapshot cleanup fail. [#170754](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170754) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@aniruddhaadak80](https://github.com/aniruddhaadak80), [@anhnt3](https://github.com/anhnt3).
- Standalone Doctor can repair matching account state when an app-owned Gateway is verified offline. [#170768](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170768) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/gateway-and-services) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@shanselman](https://github.com/shanselman).
- Expected exclusive Doctor maintenance no longer appears as a database checkpoint failure in logs or health. [#170783](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170783) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Update state inspection continues when an advisory progress message temporarily cannot be written. [#170955](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170955) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Early checks for 2026.9.2 upgrades keep database changes private so a refused update can preserve compatibility with restored code. [#171015](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171015) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@bamorem](https://github.com/bamorem).
- Doctor recognizes equivalent imported attachments instead of falsely rejecting healthy legacy session history. [#171388](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171388) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/sqlite-maintenance#changed-archived-registry) · Thanks [@he-yufeng](https://github.com/he-yufeng), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@brookpw](https://github.com/brookpw), [@junnie-stack](https://github.com/junnie-stack).
- Terminal update progress waits through temporary Doctor maintenance contention and resumes when it clears. [#171454](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171454) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Update failures during snapshot preparation identify the failed snapshot step and give matching retry guidance. [#171457](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171457) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Linux update and Doctor cleanup can reclaim abandoned updater folders despite inaccessible working directories of narrowly identified user-session processes. [#171488](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171488) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/doctor/checks) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Gateway worker setup avoids a silent successful exit when Node diagnostic flag checks meet an explicit heap limit. [#171490](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171490) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- A busy updater releases its database write lock while waiting for permission to save status, giving scheduled jobs and cleanup a chance to proceed. [#171493](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171493) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@DonnieFi](https://github.com/DonnieFi).
- Doctor and updates allow terminated child processes longer to disappear before reporting a cleanup failure. [#171497](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171497) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Updates that change core and plugins still remind users who choose --no-restart to run the appropriate Gateway restart command. [#171687](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171687) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Managed updates wait through brief maintenance overlap and preserve recovery permission so an otherwise completed update is less likely to leave the Gateway stopped. [#171699](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171699) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@fdovarela](https://github.com/fdovarela).
- State reads can resume after a database-resource cleanup failure, avoiding a Gateway restart solely to restore state-dependent work. [#171709](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171709) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@residentb2b](https://github.com/residentb2b), [@homeworld520](https://github.com/homeworld520), [@sunny251008-debug](https://github.com/sunny251008-debug).
- Doctor avoids silent empty reports when a child closes its input, while completing admitted repairs before exiting if Doctor's own output closes. [#171780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171780) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@dtump](https://github.com/dtump), [@joshpetras](https://github.com/joshpetras), [@cbhawthorne84](https://github.com/cbhawthorne84).
- Updates of standard pnpm installations find the installation's own package manager and bin folder despite the Mac Gateway service's restricted PATH. [#171936](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171936) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@AntaresGG](https://github.com/AntaresGG), [@MoerAI](https://github.com/MoerAI).
- The setup assistant diagnoses problems without applying repairs or migrations to local configuration and state. [#171960](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171960) Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Later updates can settle an unfinished completion record when the already-installed package verifies successfully. [#171993](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/171993) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@teetee2tone](https://github.com/teetee2tone).
- Updates avoid failing after a short Doctor command has already completed successfully. [#172132](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172132) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- If the new version's Doctor fails before database writes, updates can restore the previous package and databases without mistaking preliminary reads for changes. [#172151](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172151) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Updates can inspect busy agent databases without refusing solely because normal writes reuse old log space. [#172196](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172196) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- A verified update stays installed when Doctor cannot inspect its launching process, with a warning to run openclaw update repair. [#172247](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172247) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@homeworld520](https://github.com/homeworld520).
- Read-only-root Kubernetes deployments need XDG_CACHE_HOME=/home/node/.openclaw/.cache or an equivalent writable cache alongside the image update. [#172377](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172377) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/kubernetes#architecture-notes) · Thanks [@saftall](https://github.com/saftall), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Non-interactive setup preserves its actual result and credential-activation instructions when temporary credential files remain locked. [#172379](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172379) Thanks [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@giannis88](https://github.com/giannis88).
- A verified, ready Gateway update can finish with named channel warnings instead of reporting failure because a messaging channel needs attention. [#172494](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172494) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@sbc2016](https://github.com/sbc2016).
- A timed-out optional shell-completion refresh can warn without failing an otherwise successful update after cleanup is confirmed. [#172502](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172502) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Mac updates can proceed from an external login shell when native checks prove it is outside the Gateway service despite unreadable parent ancestry. [#172640](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172640) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@giulianoaccorsi](https://github.com/giulianoaccorsi).
- Linux upgrades can regenerate eligible owned legacy service files with private permissions without requiring manual chmod repair. [#172790](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172790) Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@wei887-os](https://github.com/wei887-os).
- Doctor avoids repeatedly rescanning migration manifests when stale import records refer to archived transcripts, reducing this cause of stalled repair and update checks. [#172890](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/172890) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@DongwonGim](https://github.com/DongwonGim), [@skyzhao1223](https://github.com/skyzhao1223).
- Updates and Gateway status launched through a remote node avoid mistaking the node service for the Gateway. [#173304](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173304) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [@davidcittadini](https://github.com/davidcittadini).
- Verified updates can continue with a repair warning when follow-up plugin inspection cannot finish, instead of rolling back solely for incomplete checks. [#173341](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173341) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@yoyo837](https://github.com/yoyo837).
- Updates can move past an unhealthy Gateway in the same installation with a warning, leaving time to start and fully verify its replacement. [#173358](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173358) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update/how-updates-run) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating/update-methods#source-checkout-servers-reference-script) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Gateway startup can follow a replacement model preparation instead of failing when an older preparation is cancelled. [#173366](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173366) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@Jackten](https://github.com/Jackten).
- --no-restart skips the global package updater's restart step, while a running Gateway's package watcher can still restart it after files change. [#173409](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173409) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@mwbroxson](https://github.com/mwbroxson).
- Updates wait briefly for a newly started Gateway to publish ownership before reporting restart preparation failure. [#173588](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/173588) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/install/updating) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Package updates verify the running build as well as its version; --no-restart still permits a package-watcher restart. [#174649](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/174649) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/update) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Keep scheduled workspace cleanup and worker-bundle maintenance running on paired nodes. [#168780](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168780) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/nodes/session-hosting) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).



#### Security and privacy

Approval and delivery fixes keep delayed requests tied to the person and conversation that started them. More diagnostic output masks credentials, and graphical pairing keeps them out of launcher arguments.



##### Credentials, approvals, private output, and access checks


- Supporting session integration rechecks access during compaction, membership reads, and downloads and explains unreadable device identity storage. [2770dae](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/2770dae0e6cf70bfcf617507ea20eba0ce8ef18e)
- Suggestions recheck current sharing access and session identity before returning results. [6a42f13](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/6a42f13c7ab9c3465ce578f80245b4b3493b1273)
- The first profile read after GitHub sign-in can complete while initial identity verification finishes. [90ad159](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/commit/90ad159efd8aec76b995c1c74300f126ddefb950)
- Gateway logs identify later rejected proxy sources while keeping repeated warnings bounded. [#134404](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/134404) Thanks [@lzhan011](https://github.com/lzhan011), [@abacha](https://github.com/abacha), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- PluralKit lookup failure diagnostics mask reflected credentials while retaining useful error details. [#141317](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/141317) Thanks [@hugenshen](https://github.com/hugenshen), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Plugin hooks must obtain fresh approval before changing trusted inline-approved tool arguments. [#142700](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/142700) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/hooks/tool-policy) · Thanks [@giodl73-repo](https://github.com/giodl73-repo), [@giodl73](https://github.com/giodl73).
- Switching approval-policy targets asks before discarding unsaved edits, and Cancel keeps the draft. [#149181](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/149181) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec-approvals) · Thanks [@tommyjoseph](https://github.com/tommyjoseph), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Truncated or errored replies keep recognized unfinished reasoning hidden and explain empty output-token-limit results. [#156939](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/156939) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/thinking) · Thanks [@quinszouls](https://github.com/quinszouls), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@ingenuity2k](https://github.com/ingenuity2k).
- Configured plugin tool guards run in standalone agent exec and can block commands before execution. [#159333](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/159333) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/hooks/tool-policy) · Thanks [@joseairosa](https://github.com/joseairosa), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Built-in credential masking covers secrets across long tool output while avoiding several redaction stalls and crashes. [#161480](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161480) Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
- Remote-node commands no longer fail an approval identity check when the prepared plan omitted session metadata. [#161980](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/161980) Thanks [@Program-123](https://github.com/Program-123), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@gaojian125](https://github.com/gaojian125), [@enderwillsaveyou](https://github.com/enderwillsaveyou), [@rozenpl](https://github.com/rozenpl), [@daddydubzzz](https://github.com/daddydubzzz).
- Exec-approval guidance now explains that approved command values replace later command-field edits while changed environment overrides remain rejected. [#164216](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164216) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec-approvals) · Thanks [@ericcurtin](https://github.com/ericcurtin), [@cristianoc](https://github.com/cristianoc), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Agents can ask explicitly approved peers for help and receive the reply without gaining access to their conversation histories. [#164444](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164444) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/config-tools/sessions-and-subagents#per-agent-send-only-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@prasad-yashdeep](https://github.com/prasad-yashdeep), [@cipp-ashe](https://github.com/cipp-ashe).
- Anchored conversation history rejects transcripts whose recorded owner does not match the requested conversation. [#164512](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164512) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Delayed chat startup stays bound to the current caller and conversation while initial session lookups run through workers. [#164682](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164682) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@galiniliev](https://github.com/galiniliev).
- Oversized policy-watch intervals no longer trigger near-continuous evaluations and overflow warnings. [#164850](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164850) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/policy/attestation) · Thanks [@KrasimirKralev](https://github.com/KrasimirKralev), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Scheduled commands retain valid approval through process startup and account for each approved use once, refusing uncertain or stale launches. [#164864](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164864) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec-approvals) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Covered scheduled messages and retries recheck permission at delivery handoff; revoked work cannot accept protected read results or commit an internal reply. [#164906](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/164906) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-channel-plugins) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas/worker-access) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- A2A peers with missing environment-backed credentials can no longer authenticate or send reference text as a token. [#165021](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165021) Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
- Code Mode cells cannot replace real host tool replies with forged failure results. [#165071](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165071) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/sdk-entrypoints) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/code-mode/executors) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Incognito reset safely removes its managed checkout and branch, preserving unsaved-work snapshots or reporting why a checkout was kept. [#165185](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165185) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/managed-worktrees) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/session) · Thanks [@jayzhou2309](https://github.com/jayzhou2309), [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Localized configuration guidance clarifies outbound agent messaging permissions without implying access to other sessions. [#165213](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165213)
- Restricted senders can delegate only to hidden helpers in the same agent and workspace. [#165418](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165418) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Answering or cancelling an agent question checks the current session before applying the response, refusing stale or replaced callers. [#165552](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165552) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Queued chat messages retain current sharing checks while repeated permission reads move off the Gateway thread. [#165600](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165600) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Replies with MCP app or connection buttons retain their original session-writer delivery checks. [#165760](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165760) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Tlon group-DM author claims can no longer grant owner commands or elevated permissions. Previously pending DM requests need the sender to resend after approval. [#165801](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165801) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/channels/tlon) · Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
- HTTPS through the secret egress proxy works with strict TLS clients, including modern Python on macOS; restart the Gateway and start new commands after updating. [#165818](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165818) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/secrets/secret-store-and-egress) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@vortexopenclaw](https://github.com/vortexopenclaw), [@RyanSandoval](https://github.com/RyanSandoval), [@A1fred-AI](https://github.com/A1fred-AI), [@gisk0](https://github.com/gisk0), [@jfk-hue](https://github.com/jfk-hue).
- The source-map-js dependency is updated to its patched release to address pathological source-map processing. [#165902](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/165902) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Patched MCP dependencies preserve saved credentials when background refresh encounters a different login issuer and require explicit login to switch. [#166217](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166217) Thanks [@Patrick-Erichsen](https://github.com/Patrick-Erichsen), [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc), [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Previously verified Tailscale GitHub users retain profile and session access through retryable GitHub outages. [#166254](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166254) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/user-model) · Thanks [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Approve and deny actions acknowledge saved decisions before slow notification delivery finishes. [#166396](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166396) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/protocol/rpc-devices-nodes-and-approvals) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Manual history trimming refuses requests whose access or selected session changed while older records were being restored. [#166506](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166506) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Stops canceled or no-longer-authorized worker requests before further device preparation. [#166608](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166608) Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda).
- Lets operators require a named worker profile and reject local fallback before inference. [#166611](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166611) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Applies required-worker policy to explicit destinations, moves and recovery while preserving accepted workspace results and Stop. [#166613](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166613) Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa).
- Recovers watched child runs under their recorded agent after restart and rejects coordinate actions with stale screenshots even when target references are blank. [#166691](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166691) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Node commands approved before the deadline can still run when forwarding crosses that deadline within the valid handoff window. [#166971](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/166971) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/exec-approvals) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Native Codex follow-ups are checked against the sender's permitted conversation tree, and restricted instructions match available tools. [#167075](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167075) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/plugins/codex-harness-reference/restricted-turns) · Thanks [@vincentkoc](https://github.com/vincentkoc).
- Execution-policy edits and cleanup move database writes off the Gateway main thread while current permissions remain enforced. [#167115](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167115) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Malformed text encoding in configuration files or piped patches is rejected before saved values can change. [#167118](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167118) Thanks [@Alix-007](https://github.com/Alix-007), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- LINE and Tlon mask recognizable credentials echoed in failed-request diagnostics. [#167234](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167234) Thanks [@xialonglee](https://github.com/xialonglee), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus).
- Failed exec-policy commands requested with JSON return one structured error document and a nonzero exit. [#167279](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167279) Thanks [@leon-sk668](https://github.com/leon-sk668), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
- Device commands return structured JSON refusal reasons while preserving nonzero exits and human recovery guidance. [#167294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167294) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli#json-failures) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/devices) · Thanks [@Leon-SK668](https://github.com/Leon-SK668), [@obviyus](https://github.com/obviyus), [@masatohoshino](https://github.com/masatohoshino).
- Authorized agent commands avoid spurious launch failures when existing shared-database features initialize again. [#167486](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167486) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Secrets guidance identifies unsupported command consumers before asking users to enter a protected credential. [#167976](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/167976) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/tools/secrets) · [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/cli/secrets) · Thanks [@romneyda](https://github.com/romneyda), [@markma84](https://github.com/markma84).
- Reply permission checks refuse to authorize a prepared response while its database execution remains unfinished. [#168029](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168029) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Unfinished hidden reasoning stays out of Chat Completions replies when a native tool call is followed by a token limit or content filter. [#168294](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/168294) Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete).
- Network tool results keep their untrusted-source label through Tool Search and Code Mode and into later replies. [#169560](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/169560) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/concepts/memory-architecture#trust-boundaries-and-limits) · Thanks [@RomneyDa](https://github.com/RomneyDa), [@farisoftfry](https://github.com/farisoftfry).
- Gateway exec blocks recognized variable-bearing SQLite file URLs from opening live OpenClaw state. [#170253](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170253) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/reference/database-schemas) · Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
- Doctor preserves saved OAuth profiles when an older auth file is corrupt or unreadable and warns where to inspect it. [#170256](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170256) [Docs](https://docs.openclaw.ai/gateway/doctor/state-and-sessions) · Thanks [@steipete](https://github.com/steipete), [@nosmile99](https://github.com/nosmile99).
- Graphical pairing keeps browser credentials out of launcher arguments by using private files; access to those files remains sensitive and cleanup is best effort. [#170289](https://github.com/openclaw/openclaw/pull/170289) Thanks [@eleqtrizit](https://github.com/eleqtrizit).
